Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上运行四组对比实验：基座零样本、LoRA SFT-only、RAG-only、SFT+RAG混合。使用精确匹配（章节+小节）评分，在小型人工验证待定的真实评估集上进行评估。

Result: 基座模型无法引用RTA；SFT-only误召回章节；检索能将幻觉归零；SFT+RAG混合得分最高（0.481精确匹配）且零幻觉。bge-small混合模型匹配或超越了基于更大专用检索模型的流水线；增大训练集无帮助。所有结果均未达到0.70的目标。

Conclusion: 在法规引用任务中，强力的法条引用性能不需要专用检索模型或更多数据，但当前最佳方法仍远未达到理想目标，结果被报告为初步的。

Abstract: Self-represented tenants, landlords, and help-desk staff need to be pointed at the provision of law that actually governs a question, with a correct statutory citation. We study this task on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act, 2006 (RTA) and its core regulation, asking the operator's question empirically: is fine-tuning enough, or is hybrid retrieval needed? We run a four-arm head-to-head on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base zero-shot, LoRA SFT-only, RAG-only, and an SFT+RAG hybrid), scored on citation exact-match (section+subsection) over a small, human-verification-pending real eval set. The base model cannot cite the RTA and SFT-only mis-recalls sections; retrieval is essential and drives hallucination to zero by construction; and the SFT+RAG hybrid scores highest at 0.481 exact-match with zero hallucinated citations. Its edge comes from SFT making provision selection more robust to the higher-recall candidate sets that hurt zero-shot RAG. Notably, this cheap bge-small hybrid matches or beats a pipeline built on bigger, specialized retrieval models (a larger embedder and a cross-encoder reranker), and a larger/improved training set does not help either: strong statutory-citation performance here does not require specialized retrieval models or more data. The artifact zeroes hallucination and clears the lift-over-base bar but does not reach the aspirational 0.70 exact-match target. All results are on a small, human-verification-pending real eval set and are reported as preliminary.

</details>


### [145] [Judging to Improve: A De-biased VLM-as-3D-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20364)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套鲁棒的VLM-as-3D-judge协议，并将其从排序任务扩展到优化任务。通过将评判器分为训练评判器(Qwen2.5-VL-7B)和评估评判器(InternVL3-8B)以打破循环、修正位置偏差、修复三种失败模式，实现了评判器的优化级硬化。在TRELLIS模型上对家具类别进行轻量级参数高效微调发现，独立的基础样本几乎没有可学习的偏好(0.94顺序翻转率)，信号必须通过质量对比构建。六种方法中最有效的"conditioner修复+严重退化"方案仅达到与基线持平(0.50胜率)，未突破65%胜率目标。结论是：在公开数据上使用轻量级PEFT难以超越强基线，但评判协议可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将VLM评判器从排序任务扩展到优化任务，以低成本、无人工标注的方式，将TRELLIS这样的强大开源生成模型在特定类别(家具)上进行特化。

Method: 1) 将评判器分为训练(7B)和评估(8B)两个不同模型打破循环；2) 位置偏差校正；3) 修复三种失败模式(图像过载、几何隐藏splat渲染、无参考评判奖励干净但错误输出)；4) 清晰间隙胜率0.83-1.0、基线对比~0.5的校准证据；5) 六种适应方法、两种输入模式、严重度扫描；6) 质量对比构造生成偏好信号。

Result: 1) 独立基础样本顺序翻转率0.94，几乎无可学习偏好；2) 最有效的"conditioner修复+严重退化"仅达0.50胜率(与基线持平)；3) 无方法突破65%胜率目标；4) 干净输入饱和评判器，flow-DIT微调被采样器冲刷，conditioner修复才是影响几何的关键点；5) n=8物体时胜率具有方向性。

Conclusion: 在强公开数据基线上，轻量级参数高效微调(PEFT)无法超越基线，仅能达到持平；超越基线需要比轻量级PEFT更深入的方法；所建立的评判协议是可复用的。

Abstract: A companion study established a de-biased, cross-model VLM-as-3D-judge that reliably ranks single-image-to-3D mesh quality where cheap geometry and CLIP proxies fall short. This paper asks: can that judge's preferences specialize a strong open generator, TRELLIS, on one asset class (furniture), cheaply and without human labels? Taking the judge from ranking to optimization is where the work lives. Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [146] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAX，一个基于JAX的高效安全强化学习基准测试平台，利用MJX物理引擎实现约100倍加速，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，评估6种安全RL方法发现无单一方法占优，并展示了课程学习与安全迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL基准测试在高保真3D物理环境中计算速度慢，限制了大规模实验和快速原型开发，亟需一个加速的基准平台。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎构建，利用JAX的向量化操作和硬件加速（GPU/TPU）实现高速仿真，设计6个环境套件和3个智能体任务（各含3个难度级别）。

Result: 相比基于CPU的安全基准测试实现约100倍加速；6种流行安全RL方法评估显示无单一方法在所有任务中占优，存在性能与安全性的权衡；课程学习与安全迁移在困难设置中优于直接训练。

Conclusion: CRAX大幅提升了安全RL基准测试的效率，揭示了现有方法的性能-安全权衡，并为未来研究提供了高效、可扩展的评估平台。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [147] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态联邦图学习中的客户端级和节点级模态不平衡问题，提出FedMGS框架，通过隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成范式恢复缺失模态语义，集成可用性感知图编码器、原型引导的潜在语义合成器和可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在四个任务上最高提升17.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习(MM-FGL)在实际部署中面临两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战：客户端级别（某些客户端缺失整个模态）和节点级别（单个节点缺失视觉或文本属性）。现有研究主要针对图无关或集中式场景，难以直接适配。

Method: 将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成问题，提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；(2) 原型引导的潜在语义合成器，建立跨客户端的语义锚点；(3) 可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在预测读出前调节恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上的大量实验表明，FedMGS持续优于竞争基线，最高提升达17.41%，且实现了最佳效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效解决多模态联邦图学习中模态不平衡问题的方法，通过在表示空间中直接恢复缺失模态语义，最大化与原始数据语义分布的对齐，缓解缺失模态带来的高方差问题。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [148] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无需人工标注的意图分类合成对话数据生成框架，仅依赖意图定义，通过主题和风格属性提升数据多样性，并设计了两种后置风格化模型（Univ和Exam）以及LLM-as-a-judge过滤流程。实验表明该方法能达到使用人工标注数据性能的93.3%，且发现风格多样性比主题多样性更重要，生成过程中融入风格属性比后置风格适配更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速迭代的工业场景中，意图分类所需的带标注种子数据难以获取，传统方法依赖人工标注，成本高且效率低。因此需要一种完全无需人工标注数据、仅依赖意图定义就能生成高质量合成对话数据的框架。

Method: 1）提出基于两种不同类型主题和风格属性的对话生成框架，提升数据多样性；2）设计两种后置风格化模型Univ和Exam，将LLM生成的合成话语转换为更丰富、更像人类的语言风格；3）采用LLM-as-a-judge过滤机制提升数据质量。

Result: 在工业数据集和公开数据集上，该方法能达到使用人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%。此外，风格多样性对合成数据效用的重要性高于主题多样性；在生成过程中融入风格属性比后置风格适配效果更好。

Conclusion: 提出了一种完全无需人工标注数据的合成对话数据生成框架，验证了风格多样性在合成数据中比主题多样性更关键，并揭示了生成阶段融入风格属性优于后置风格适配，为工业场景下的意图分类提供了有效的数据生成方案。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [149] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计（DAE）到部分可观测领域，并通过引入离散潜动态模型降低计算复杂度，在ALE基准上验证了其样本效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAE方法在深度强化学习中能提升样本效率，但其依赖完全可观测环境且需建模转移概率导致计算开销大，限制了在现实场景中的应用。

Method: 将DAE理论框架扩展至部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），引入离散潜动态模型高效近似转移概率，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上，DAE方法随函数逼近器容量扩展而有效提升性能，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论扩展和计算优化，使得DAE适用于更实际的强化学习场景，兼顾了可扩展性与样本效率。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [150] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对深度神经网络在信息物理系统中应对虚假数据注入攻击的防御框架，通过在输入样本中添加基于统计分布的伪特征填充，以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使对抗攻击在计算上不可行，同时保持轻量级、模型无关且无需修改核心架构。在IEEE多母线电力系统上的实验验证了该方法有效提升模型鲁棒性，且对性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在信息物理系统中用于检测虚假数据注入攻击时，由于CPS独特的基础设施易被攻击者利用以逃避检测，且传统防御机制难以应对这类攻击。

Method: 提出一个创新的防御框架，在DNN的额外输入层中对输入样本进行填充，填充值基于输入统计分布生成的伪特征，以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使对抗扰动因不可迁移和填充结构不可预测而变得计算上不可行。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、118和300母线系统上的对抗性实验表明，该填充策略显著提升了模型鲁棒性，对性能影响极小，且有效缓解了传统防御无法阻止的攻击。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心架构，在真实CPS场景中具有高部署性，能有效增强DNN对FDIA的防御能力。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [151] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明扩散模型中的显式时间步嵌入并非必要，在特定条件下无时间条件模型可达到同等甚至更优性能，挑战了长期以来的时间条件范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，探究U-Net和Diffusion Transformer架构是否能在去除时间信号的情况下依然有效工作。

Method: 提供理论框架证明全局最小化扩散训练目标可在无显式时间条件下实现；在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融实验，比较有无时间嵌入的性能差异。

Result: 无时间条件模型在FID、precision和recall等指标上可维持高结构保真度，甚至超越有条件模型；架构可从含噪输入中隐式推断噪声尺度。

Conclusion: 显式时间条件化是冗余的，扩散架构具备隐式感知噪声尺度的能力，为更高效、聚焦结构的生成模型开辟新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [152] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种用于微分方程逆问题的不确定性量化神经代理模型，通过延迟接受MCMC诊断代理后验的保真度，在高维参数空间中相比高斯过程代理保持更高效率，并能融入微分方程约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法求解微分方程逆问题（尤其是贝叶斯框架下）计算成本高昂，正向模型复杂且参数空间高维时似然评估代价大，亟需可扩展且可靠的不确定性感知代理模型。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络代理替代微分方程求解器；使用延迟接受MCMC的短运行作为代理后验近似保真度的诊断工具；在数值实验中与高斯过程代理进行对比，并验证对微分方程约束的兼容性。

Result: DeepGaLA在正向模型近似精度上与高斯过程代理相当，但在参数维度增长时能更好地维持计算效率；可有效融入非线性微分方程约束；延迟接受MCMC可作为有效的诊断工具。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经代理模型（如DeepGaLA）能够为复杂系统中的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [153] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可控的玩具世界框架，利用生成器-分离器流水线、可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务设计，以及基于SINDy的保留动态分析和有效秩分析，揭示了持续学习中遗忘机制的关键因素：叠加（特征方向重叠）随时间增加但会在任务边界出现短暂下降；高特征稀疏性虽增加叠加但不必然导致遗忘；任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长。这些发现修正了"叠加越多遗忘越多"的直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统经常遗忘先前获得的知识，但由于真实数据集纠缠了多种因素，导致遗忘机制在实践中难以隔离和研究。

Method: 提出了一个可控的玩具世界框架：使用生成器-分离器流水线定义真实潜在特征；构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务；引入表示强度和叠加（特征方向重叠）的可测量量；通过SINDy（稀疏动态关系拟合）研究保留动态；采用基于有效秩的任务级分析表征能力分配。

Result: (1) 叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现短暂下降，表明边界特定干扰而非稳态漂移；(2) 高特征稀疏性诱导更多叠加，但不必然导致遗忘；当表示强度足够强时，即使存在重叠也能减少遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏机制下能力使用更广泛。

Conclusion: 叠加（重叠）与表示强度和容量分配之间存在交互作用，修正了"叠加越多遗忘越多"的常见直觉；该玩具分析提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具用于持续学习研究。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [154] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASYS是一个基于智能体的符号搜索框架，通过将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的可微分符号程序，结合进化搜索与梯度优化，自动发现PDE的解析解或解析近似解。在五个基准问题上，ASYS成功恢复了已知解析形式，并为之前无闭式描述的问题（如Allen-Cahn 2D几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破解的九参数收缩律）构造了可解释的解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表格理解PDE解，但传统解析分析需逐个手工推导，数值模拟和神经网络无法直接产生这些数学结构。因此需要一个自动化框架来发现PDE解的解析表示形式。

Method: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，将PDE理论、问题约束和历史搜索经验编码为可测试的可微分符号程序。符号形式的搜索采用进化策略，连续参数通过梯度优化拟合，将搜索过程转变为自动化的归纳偏差注入而非盲目的符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS恢复了已知解析解，并为Allen-Cahn 2D动力学发现了几何界面公式，为Keller-Segel趋化爆破解发现了九参数收缩律，这些之前均无闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式可能性，超越了手工解析解、基于网格的数值解和神经网络近似，能够自动生成可解释的解析表示以指导数学家的进一步分析。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [155] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fisher信息矩阵的黎曼几何将"扁平最小值"形式化为黎曼锐度（SR），证明了该度量在保持函数的重参数化下具有不变性，并从理论上证明了SGD隐式偏好SR较小的平坦最小值，从而建立了泛化性能与几何平坦性之间的严格联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧几里得度量下的损失Hessian矩阵的平坦性度量（如迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数的重参数化下不具有不变性，这破坏了"SGD偏好平坦最小值且平坦最小值泛化更好"这一直觉的理论基础。本文旨在解决这一根本性矛盾。

Method: 1）将平坦性定义在由Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何上，定义黎曼锐度（Riemannian Sharpness, SR）；2）证明SR在光滑、保持函数的重参数化下具有不变性；3）将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为协方差结构与FIM成比例的分布，推导对应的随机微分方程的平稳分布，证明概率质量指数级集中在SR较小的平坦最小值；4）建立受SR显式控制的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明：SR能够可靠地追踪泛化性能，而欧几里得锐度（Hessian迹/最大特征值）则不能；SR随η/B的缩放行为与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个严格的、重参数化不变的关于"扁平最小值为何泛化更好"的理论解释，解决了Dinh等人提出的"尖锐最小值也能泛化"的批判。但需注意，实际使用的对角经验估计器仅近似具有不变性，精确不变性需要结构化估计器（如K-FAC）。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [156] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向多轮对话代理工作负载的4位KV缓存压缩技术，提出了UltraQuant方法，在AMD GPU上相比FP8 KV缓存基线实现了3.47倍的P50首Token延迟降低和1.63倍的输出吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文密集的代理场景中，长前缀被多轮短对话复用，KV缓存压力巨大，同时并发性决定了GPU利用率。现有FP8 KV缓存在高并发长上下文场景下存在性能瓶颈，亟需更高效的4位量化方案来平衡任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐。

Method: 1) 使用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点；2) 提出非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除和块缩放变体等设计选择；3) 在AMD GPU上优化解码注意力核，提出UltraQuant——FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放和CDNA4原生缩放MFMA支持。

Result: 在长上下文多轮代理工作负载上：P50首Token延迟在缓存压力大的后期轮次降低3.47倍，所有轮次平均降低2.3倍；输出吞吐量相比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4位KV缓存压缩在代理工作负载中具有显著优势，UltraQuant通过精心设计的量化策略和硬件适配，在任务质量、缓存效率和吞吐性能之间取得了更优的权衡。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [157] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边际优势累积（MAA）方法，通过跨批次操作级证据累积机制解决批量式轨迹蒸馏中反馈矛盾的问题，在16个设置中取得14个最佳结果，并节省约75%的优化阶段token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有批量式轨迹蒸馏方法缺乏跨批次、操作级别的证据累积机制，导致同一内存操作在不同批次中收到矛盾反馈，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中。

Method: 将需求形式化为对齐性（alignability）和可比性（comparability）两个结构条件；提出MAA方法，通过构建差分信号使信号跨批次可比，利用指数移动平均（EMA）累积每个操作的有符号证据，并通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: MAA作为后处理架构，在4个基准和4个目标模型的16个设置中取得14个最佳结果，持续优于现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中达到或超越在线方法，并将优化阶段token消耗降低约75%。

Conclusion: MAA通过跨批次操作级证据累积有效解决了轨迹蒸馏中的反馈矛盾问题，显著提升性能并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [158] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"传染网络"框架，研究大语言模型作为评估者时，其评估偏差如何在多智能体系统中传播。实验发现：同模型智能体间偏差传播系数γ∈[0.157,0.352]，比跨模型传播弱3-5倍；将评估委员会从1人增至3人可减少72.4%的偏差传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为评估者时，其系统性评估偏差会在多智能体网络中传播，但缺乏正式的量化框架来测量和理解这一传播过程。

Method: 提出"传染网络"形式化框架，通过跨智能体传染矩阵Γ_N测量偏差传播；使用DeepSeek-chat设计3种偏差画像（结构化、均衡、基于证据）的三智能体实验；计算谱半径ρ(Γ_N)识别传播机制。

Result: 同模型智能体间偏差传播系数γ∈[0.157,0.352]，比跨模型传播弱3-5倍；评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 同模型智能体处于"抑制机制"；增大评估委员会规模是有效的缓解策略；传染网络框架为评估偏差传播提供了形式化工具。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [159] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules）机制，一种针对低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的图绑定检查点与恢复机制。FlashRT系统通过捕获完整执行状态（包括KV缓存、循环状态、卷积状态等）实现亚毫秒级的GPU快照与恢复，在RTX 5090上TTFT相比冷启动预填充加速3.9倍至27倍，为KV缓存重用提供了互补的低延迟服务方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM服务系统主要通过分页或基数KV缓存重用前缀工作，这在高吞吐、高并发场景下高效，但仅管理KV缓存这一单一位置片段。然而在低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务场景中（如交互式LLM代理、语音系统、机器人策略），需要频繁分支、重置、中断和重新进入，现有KV缓存机制无法满足严格的响应时间预算要求。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种图绑定（graph-bound）的检查点与恢复机制，在提交边界处保存完整可恢复状态。FlashRT是一个白盒、后端面向内核的运行时，其NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活动状态是一组封闭的命名缓冲区，胶囊可以对整个执行边界（包括KV、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据）进行快照、恢复、分支或回滚。将重用从基于token地址的KV片段转移到基于图绑定的执行状态边界。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别是字节精确的，在贪心解码下是token一致的。仅KV的消融实验出现发散，表明循环状态是关键的。GPU驻留的快照和恢复时间在亚毫秒级别，TTFT加速比从2k tokens的3.9倍增长到16k tokens的27倍。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上同样验证了正确性和结构特性。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊并非替代高吞吐KV缓存服务的方案，而是定义了一种互补的、以延迟为首要目标的服务点，专门用于显式的执行状态重用场景。该机制特别适合设备端物理AI服务中需要频繁状态切换的低延迟交互场景。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销层次贝叶斯预测推断。该方法将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，训练transformer模型学习跨先验族进行自适应预测，在多项评估中匹配贝叶斯预言机的同时速度快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推断虽然理论完备，但精确推断难以实现，可扩展近似方法要么计算昂贵，要么需要限制性假设而损害预测性能。现有先验数据拟合模型和上下文模型与训练先验支持紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的显式机制，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架，将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix）；训练transformer模型处理由先验任务和目标任务组成的序列，使其学会跨不同先验族进行自适应的预测推断。

Result: 在难度递增的多项评估（包括元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验）中，该方法匹配了贝叶斯预言机（oracle）的性能，同时计算速度提升数个数量级。在真实世界的时空温度预测基准上也展示了实际效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能有效适应不同先验的摊销贝叶斯预测推断方法，兼顾了贝叶斯方法的不确定性量化优势和计算效率，为在分布偏移下保持鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [161] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可预测性隐私"（privacy via predictability）框架，通过衡量攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增益来量化隐私泄露，与差分隐私（DP）形成互补，并基于广义矩估计（GMM）建立了渐近分析框架，提出了可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私的 worst-case 性质导致隐私-精度权衡代价过高，需要一种更细粒度的隐私度量框架，能够显式地融入攻击者的先验知识（被泄露的部分数据）以及对特定敏感信息和查询族的隐私保护需求。

Method: 1）定义"可预测性"隐私度量，衡量攻击者在观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增益；2）证明可预测性与DP在一般情况下不可比较（互不蕴含）；3）在极端场景（仅剩一个个体未被泄露，所有二值查询均为敏感）下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP；4）利用广义矩估计（GMM）建立稳态遍历混合过程下的渐近可预测性分析框架；5）基于该分析提出可预测性校准的输出扰动方案用于经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 可预测性与DP在一般情况下不可比较，但在极端条件下可预测性可蕴含互信息DP；可预测性提供了更细粒度的隐私度量，能够针对特定敏感信息和攻击者模型进行定制；基于GMM的渐近分析有效，校准后的输出扰动方案可行。

Conclusion: 可预测性隐私框架是差分隐私的补充工具，能提供更细粒度的隐私控制，可与DP联合使用以优化隐私-精度权衡。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [162] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，提出了达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并将其推广到结果不可区分性、全能预测和泛预测等应用中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准是可信机器学习的基本要求，此前达到极小极大最优样本复杂度（Õ(ε⁻³)）的预测器都是随机化的，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度显著更差。随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要是一个公开问题，本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 提出一个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的多校准算法，输出确定性预测器；然后将算法推广到满足结果不可区分性（OI）的确定性预测器，以及用于有限或有限覆盖的测试集合。

Result: 解决了随机化是否对多校准最优样本复杂度必要的公开问题，给出了最优确定性预测器算法；同时得到了最优样本复杂度的确定性全能预测器和泛预测器，解决了[OKK25]和[BHHLZ25]的开放问题。

Conclusion: 随机化并非多校准最优样本复杂度所必需，确定性预测器也可以达到相同的极小极大最优率。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [163] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DiffusionGemma（一种扩散语言模型）的推理透明性，发现其虽然初始不透明序列深度是自回归Gemma 4的28.6倍，但通过可解释的token瓶颈可将不透明深度降至仅1.1倍；算法透明性方面扩散模型更难，但通过案例研究发现了非时间顺序推理等新现象；下游可监控性与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）在连续潜空间中执行大部分计算是否会导致推理透明性降低，从而理解模型决策、缓解误用和对齐问题、调试异常行为。

Method: 将透明性分解为变量透明性（理解中间计算状态）和算法透明性（重构推理过程）。通过可解释token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间信息流，并开展可解释性案例研究。

Result: 变量透明性：通过token瓶颈将不透明序列深度从28.6倍降至1.1倍；算法透明性：发现了非时间顺序推理、token和序列涂抹、中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象；可监控性：与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的推理透明性虽面临挑战，但通过适当方法可大幅提升，且在下游可监控性方面与自回归模型表现相似。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Simulating Robotic Locomotion in Sand: Resistive Force Theory in an Open-Source Physics Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19504)
*Ryan Walker Brown,Laura K. Treers,Kathryn A. Daltorio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒电阻力理论（3D RFT），用于近似模拟机器人在沙地行走时的地面反作用力。实验验证表明，该方法能在20%误差范围内预测六足机器人在沙地上的行走距离和脚部沉陷量，为机器人穿越颗粒介质的设计提供了高效仿真工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFT方法可近似计算沙地 locomotion 的地面反作用力，但尚未集成到常用的3D物理仿真引擎中，限制了机器人设计中对颗粒介质行走的仿真能力。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现3D颗粒电阻力理论（3D RFT），将其与标准动力学计算集成，并通过多场景仿真验证末端执行器形状、速度和负载等关键趋势。

Result: 该实现能在20%误差范围内预测12自由度六足机器人在沙地上的行走距离和脚部沉陷量，并准确保持末端执行器形状、速度和负载的关键趋势。

Conclusion: 尽管RFT存在固有近似，但该开源工具有潜力帮助开发和改进穿越颗粒介质基底的机器人设计。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Resistive Force Theory (RFT) enable approximation of ground reaction forces for locomotion in sand without the computational expense of modeling interactions with individual grains. However, these tools have been absent in 3D physics engines commonly used for robot simulation. We explore if resistive force approximations are sufficient, when integrated with standard dynamics calculations, to provide a stable substrate for a freely walking robot. To determine this, we implement 3D Granular Resistive Force Theory (3D RFT) in a physics simulation engine, MuJoCo. We verify simulations in multiple scenarios to demonstrate that key trends due to end effector shape, speed, and loading are preserved. Our implementation predicts walking distance and foot sinkage of a 12-Degree of Freedom hexapod robot within 20\% of experiments in sand. While RFT has inherent approximations, the open source tool described here has potential to help develop new and improved robot designs to traverse granular media substrates.

</details>


### [165] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Physical Atari系统，包括Robotroller机械臂和Atari Devbox显示设备，用于在物理世界中研究强化学习算法。该系统成本低于1000美元，可连续运行数周无机械故障，并验证了强化学习算法可直接在机器人上学习，同时发现学习与部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个鲁棒、可访问的物理平台，用于在真实世界中研究强化学习算法，弥合模拟与物理世界之间的差距。

Method: 设计Robotroller（通过轴承驱动Atari CX40+控制器）和Atari Devbox（渲染游戏画面和奖励信号），配合摄像头和桌面计算机组成完整系统；采用轴承减少磨损，高频伺服状态监控以限制应力，使用低成本商用组件和3D打印部件。

Result: 系统成本低于1000美元，可连续数周不间断运行无机械故障；验证了强化学习算法可直接在机器人上学习；发现学习与部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: Physical Atari是一个鲁棒且可访问的物理强化学习研究平台，实验结果强调了设备上适应性对于机器人在实际部署中取得强性能的重要性。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [166] [Proprioceptive Invariant State Estimation for Humanoid Robots on Non-Inertial Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19512)
*Falak Mandali,Zijian He,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）的方法，利用足部IMU的 proprioceptive 传感和运动学约束，在非惯性地面上对仿人机器人进行实时状态估计，无需外部传感器或地面运动测量。实验表明，该方法在收敛速度和位置估计精度上显著优于现有 InEKF 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人状态估计方法大多假设地面为惯性系，当机器人在移动地面（如船舶、车辆等）上运行时，传统方法会因地面运动引入的非线性而失效。本文旨在解决非惯性地面下仅依赖本体感知进行实时状态估计的挑战。

Method: 1) 采用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）框架；2) 利用足部惯性测量单元（IMU）的运动学约束来建模地面诱导的非线性过程与测量模型；3) 公式化为右不变测量模型，以在大初始不确定性下获得良好的误差动态特性；4) 进行了可观测性分析，确定了相对位姿和速度的可观测条件。

Result: 在Digit仿人机器人上，站立和蹲起于摇摆俯仰地面时：收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%。在单轴旋转地面上行走时，初始误差高达1米时，平均估计误差小于9厘米。

Conclusion: 所提的InEKF方法在非惯性地面场景下显著优于现有InEKF方法，仅依靠本体感知即可实现快速收敛和精确估计，为人形机器人在动态平台上（如船舶、移动车辆）的自主操作提供了可行的状态估计方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an invariant extended Kalman filtering (InEKF) approach for real-time state estimation of humanoid robots operating on non-inertial ground using only onboard proprioceptive sensing. The proposed approach estimates the robot's base position and velocity relative to the moving ground frame without requiring direct measurements of ground motion or externally mounted sensors. By exploiting kinematic constraints at the stance foot through foot-mounted IMUs, the filter accounts for ground-induced nonlinearities in the process and measurement models while remaining fully proprioceptive. The estimator is formulated to admit a right-invariant measurement model, enabling favorable error dynamics under large initial uncertainties. Observability analysis establishes conditions under which the robot's relative base position and velocity are observable with respect to the non-inertial ground frame. Experiments with the Digit humanoid robot standing and squatting atop a swaying and pitching ground showcase a 96% speedup in convergence rate and an 80% reduction in position estimate errors over existing InEKFs. Walking experiments on a uni-axially rotating ground achieve an average estimation error of less than 9 cm for an initial error of up to 1 m.

</details>


### [167] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WorkBenchMark，一个基于乐高得宝的机器人组装基准测试，包含400个任务和四个复杂度层级，并提供了基于规划的开源基线方案，在各项指标上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端学习方法未能可靠解决机器人组装中低层操作与物理约束下任务级符号推理的结合问题，因此需要建立标准化的基准测试来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了基于乐高得宝的400个组装任务（四个复杂度层级），提出Assembly-by-Disassembly的开放词汇感知基线方案，采用基于规划的流水线方法。

Result: 基于规划的流水线方案在所有复杂度层级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。

Conclusion: 该基准测试及开源实现将为机器人装配社区提供标准化评估平台，促进相关研究发展。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [168] [pdSTL: Probabilistic Differentiable Signal Temporal Logic for Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19561)
*Bennett Dogbey,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出概率可微时序逻辑(pdSTL)，将概率语义与可微鲁棒性统一，用于不确定环境下自主机器人的轨迹优化，实验验证其优于确定性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL扩展要么缺乏可微性，要么忽略信念空间不确定性，无法同时满足梯度优化需求和概率安全保障。

Method: 1) 采用区间值概率语义计算保守满足界，组合式传播通过STL语法树；2) 将时序鲁棒性评估建模为LSTM风格递归展开，实现线性时间可微监控。

Result: 在仿真避障、变道和真实Crazyflie四旋翼飞行实验中，pdSTL在保持安全裕度方面显著优于确定性可微STL。

Conclusion: pdSTL在不确定环境下实现了高效的优化与形式化概率保证，适用于端到端轨迹优化。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in uncertain environments must satisfy complex temporal and safety specifications despite stochastic dynamics and sensing noise. While Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers robustness measures for gradient-based optimization, existing extensions either lack differentiability or ignore belief-space uncertainty. We introduce pdSTL (probabilistic differentiable Signal Temporal Logic), a framework that unifies probabilistic semantics with differentiable robustness over belief trajectories. pdSTL employs interval-valued probabilistic semantics to compute conservative satisfaction bounds, propagated compositionally through the STL syntax tree. We formulate the temporal robustness evaluation as a recurrent, LSTM-style unfolding of STL operators, enabling linear-time, differentiable monitoring suitable for end-to-end trajectory optimization. We validate pdSTL on simulated obstacle avoidance, lane-change maneuvers, and real-world Crazyflie quadcopter flight experiments under aerodynamic disturbances. Results demonstrate that pdSTL achieves efficient optimization with formal probabilistic guarantees, significantly outperforming deterministic differentiable STL in maintaining safety margins under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [169] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对3D场景图（3DSG）进行了统一且批判性的综述，重点分析了其形式化定义、建模选择、构建管线、下游应用及评估策略，并指出了该领域碎片化现状及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSG作为一种结合几何基础与语义关系抽象的强大空间AI表示，已被广泛应用于机器人学和计算机视觉的诸多问题。然而，该领域存在碎片化问题——不同社区采用不同的定义、构建流程和评估协议，导致方法难以比较、共同假设难以识别、实际部署中的挑战难以评估。本文旨在提供统一且批判性的综述以解决这些问题。

Method: 首先在统一定义下形式化3DSG，分析节点和边属性、层次结构、动态场景表示、可供性扩展等建模选择；然后综述从原始传感器观测构建3DSG的常用术语、约定和技术；最后考察下游应用和评估策略，涵盖内在图质量到任务级性能。

Result: 建立了3DSG的统一形式化定义，系统梳理了不同建模选择与构建技术，识别了该领域的碎片化问题，并指出了开放挑战和未来方向。

Conclusion: 3DSG是一个极具表现力但尚处于碎片化阶段的研究领域，需要更统一的定义、构建和评估标准才能实现稳健的实际部署。本文为该领域提供了全面的参考框架。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [170] [Safe, Real-Time Active Model Discrimination and Fault Diagnosis for Nonlinear Systems via Differentiable Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19590)
*Xinpei Ni,Melkior Ornik,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对不确定连续时间非线性系统的安全、实时的主动故障诊断与模型辨别算法，通过区间过近似和可微目标函数实现快速在线优化，在多种机器人平台上验证了其高效性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动故障诊断方法在面对非线性和不确定性系统时，难以在实时性、安全性和可扩展性之间取得平衡，尤其是在高维系统中缺乏兼顾形式化安全保证与计算效率的解决方案。

Method: 将问题建模为输出反馈时变策略优化问题，利用区间过近似（interval over-approximation）对可达状态和输出集进行保守估计，设计可微目标函数惩罚候选模型可达输出集的重叠程度，并使用JAX和可微可达性原语进行高效的基于梯度的在线求解。

Result: 在四旋翼、战斗机模型、差速驱动机器人和四足机器人导航等多种高维非线性系统中（最多11种故障模式），该方法在50毫秒内实现可靠的模型辨别，在辨别成功率和速度上均超越基线方法，同时提供形式化安全保证。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了非线性不确定系统下主动故障诊断的安全性与实时性挑战，为机器人安全关键系统的在线故障辨识提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We present a safe, real-time algorithm for active fault diagnosis and model discrimination for uncertain continuous-time nonlinear systems with process and measurement disturbances. Given a finite set of candidate models representing nominal and faulty modes, including actuator and sensor faults, we formulate an output-feedback, time-varying policy optimization problem that (i) robustly enforces state-input safety constraints over a finite horizon and (ii) drives the system to produce sampled measurements consistent with at most one model, enabling deterministic diagnosis. To solve this problem in real time, we develop a tractable approximation using interval over-approximations of reachable state and output sets, and encode diagnosability via a differentiable objective that penalizes overlap between the reachable output sets of possible models. The resulting optimization is solved efficiently online with gradient-based methods using JAX and differentiable reachability primitives. We evaluate our method on sensor and actuator fault diagnosis (up to 11 fault modes) in several high-dimensional nonlinear robotic systems, including a simulated quadrotor and fighter-jet model, a hardware differential-drive robot, and quadrupedal navigation. Across these case studies, our approach achieves reliable model discrimination in under 50 ms, outperforming baselines in discrimination success rate and speed while providing formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [171] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散策略的视觉伺服方法，使用归一化标签角点坐标作为输入，通过条件去噪生成相机速度，并采用在线训练范式持续扩展数据多样性，在仿真和实际实验中分别达到近100%和93%的成功率，同时验证了扩散机制可泛化提升现有视觉伺服网络的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归式视觉伺服方法存在两个主要问题：对噪声敏感的单步映射导致轨迹抖动，以及分布偏移时误差累积。作者希望通过扩散策略的动作序列预测能力和隐式数据增广来克服这些局限。

Method: 基于扩散策略，以观测到的标签角点归一化图像坐标为输入，通过条件去噪过程生成相机速度。为克服静态数据集训练的泛化局限，采用在线训练范式，通过交互式经验收集持续扩大训练数据多样性。

Result: 仿真实验成功率近100%，实际物理实验成功率93%。此外，将扩散模块集成到现有视觉伺服网络中，均能一致地提升性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散策略具有广泛适用性，不仅能用于特定流程，还能增强多种视觉伺服系统的性能。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [172] [Comparative Study on Agility, Efficiency, and Impact Absorption of Bipedal Robots with Active Toes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19699)
*Joong-Gil Kim,Wontae Ye,Geunwoo Cho,Seong-Ho Yun,Se-Hyoung Cho,Yong-Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种14自由度仿人双足机器人，通过高保真仿真环境对比有无主动脚趾配置的性能。实验表明，在1.33m/s行走速度下，带脚趾的机器人比无脚趾配置降低了17.5%的运输成本和5.0%的冲击力，路径偏差减少25%-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类脚趾在行走中具有高效、敏捷和减震的关键作用，但现有机器人未能充分复现人类脚趾特性，也缺乏对其优势的严格量化验证。

Method: 设计14自由度双足机器人模拟人类脚趾轻量、高扭矩、耐用的特性；构建包含耦合传动和精确功耗的高保真仿真训练环境；设计最小化强化学习奖励函数，对有无主动脚趾的配置采用相同训练流程进行公平对比。

Result: 带脚趾配置在1.33m/s行走时：运输成本降低17.5%，足跟着地冲击力减少5.0%，平均路径偏差降低25.0%，最大路径偏差降低34.0%。

Conclusion: 主动脚趾能显著提升双足机器人的能效、减震性能和敏捷性，验证了仿人脚趾在足式机器人中的关键作用。

Abstract: Human legs exhibit high efficiency, agility, and impact absorption, with toes playing a crucial role in these capabilities. While many attempts have been made to implement human-like toes in robots, they have not fully replicated human characteristics nor rigorously validated their benefits. We propose a 14-DOF biped robot emulating human toes' lightweight, high-torque, robust nature. To quantitatively analyze the effectiveness of the active toes in terms of agility, efficiency, and impact absorption, we developed a high-fidelity simulation training environment that reflects actual actuators with coupled transmissions and accurate power consumption. To ensure a fair comparison between configurations with and without active toes, we designed a minimal RL reward function and applied an identical training procedure to both. The simulation results indicate that, at 1.33 m/s walking, the toe-equipped robot reduced CoT by 17.5% and heel-strike GRF by 5.0% compared with the toe-ablation configuration. On the agility test, average and maximum path deviation decreased by 25.0% and 34.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [173] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RATs框架，让具身编码智能体通过自导向"玩耍"阶段自主探索、执行和蒸馏技能，构建可复用的代码技能库，在下游任务中无需微调即可提升性能，在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上分别取得20.6和17.0个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体系统虽能编写代码策略并依据反馈迭代修正，但本质仍是任务驱动的——可复用技能仅在有明确指令时才能习得。作者希望让智能体在任务到来前通过自导向的"玩耍"阶段持续学习技能，实现无监督的技能积累。

Method: 提出RATs（机器人智能体团队），在玩耍阶段：1) 自主提出新颖且可学习的探索任务；2) 规划并执行机器人-代码策略；3) 验证中间进度；4) 诊断失败并利用密集的步骤级反馈重试；5) 将成功执行的策略蒸馏为持久化的代码技能库。测试时从冻结的库中检索相关技能辅助新任务求解。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上，玩耍学习的技能相比无玩耍和随机玩耍基线分别提升20.6和17.0个百分点；学习到的技能无需微调即可插入其他推理时Code-as-Policy智能体，在RoboSuite和真实世界迁移中分别提升8.9和8.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 自导向的玩耍式技能学习能有效提升下游任务性能，且学到的技能具有可迁移性和即插即用特性，为具身智能体的持续自主学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively. Moreover, the learned skills can be plugged into other inference-time Code-as-Policy agents by simply retrieving them into the context, improving RoboSuite and real-world transfer by 8.9 and 8.8 points, respectively, without finetuning the underlying model.

</details>


### [174] [A Differentiable Composite Approximation Framework for Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Maneuvering Modeling from Sea-Trial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19711)
*Aobo Wang,Aifei Xia,Zihao Wang,Lizhu Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微复合逼近方法，将多项式基函数与数据自适应基函数（神经网络）联合校准，并引入转向运动电流估计与补偿机制，用于基于现场数据的AUV操纵建模。海试数据验证表明，该方法在递归轨迹与速度预测上优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AUV操纵建模方法中，传统方法使用预定义多项式基函数，而数据驱动方法使用数据自适应基函数，二者各有优劣。本文受基函数视角启发，旨在将两者融合为统一的可微复合逼近模型，实现联合校准以提升基于现场测量数据的AUV操纵预测精度。

Method: 提出可微复合逼近公式，将多项式基分量与数据自适应基分量（神经残差）作为同一预测器的可微部分进行联合校准（梯度协同校准）；设计敏感度感知机制调控有界多项式更新；引入基于转向运动的电流估计与补偿方法，构建电流补偿学习目标用于训练与 rollout。

Result: 使用7米长AUV多操纵条件下海试数据评估，所提方法在递归轨迹与速度预测上均优于纯多项式、纯神经网络及冻结先验混合基线模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出的可微复合逼近联合校准框架，有效结合了物理先验（多项式）与数据驱动（神经网络）的优势，并通过电流补偿机制提升了对现场海洋环境数据的适应能力，验证了其在基于现场数据的AUV操纵建模中的实用性。

Abstract: Field-based modeling from onboard measurements can produce autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) maneuvering models that reflect real operating characteristics. From an approximation perspective, conventional maneuvering models use predefined constraint polynomial bases, whereas data-driven models use data-adaptive bases. Motivated by this basis-function view, this paper presents a differentiable composite-approximation formulation, in which the polynomial-basis component and the data-adaptive basis component are treated as differentiable parts of a single predictor and calibrated jointly. A gradient-based co-calibration method is developed for full-scale AUV maneuvering prediction, where a sensitivity-aware mechanism regulates bounded polynomial updates while the neural residual captures remaining nonlinear discrepancies under a shared prediction objective. To account for ocean-current effects in field data, a turning-motion-based current estimation and compensation procedure is incorporated to construct current-compensated learning targets for training and rollout. The framework is evaluated using sea-trial data collected from a 7-meter AUV under multiple maneuvering conditions. Results show that the proposed method improves recursive trajectory and velocity prediction compared with polynomial-only, neural-only, and frozen-prior hybrid baselines, demonstrating its applicability to field-data-based AUV maneuvering modeling.

</details>


### [175] [A Categorial and Sheaf-Theoretic Semantics for Autonomic Component Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19525)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出用范畴论和层论为SCEL（软件组件集合语言）构建多层数学模型，将机器人社会建模为拓扑空间上的层，把信息共享形式化为层的"粘合"操作，系统故障视为拓扑障碍并通过层上同调量化，从而将分布式系统的验证转化为数学对象的几何分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模去中心化自主系统（如机器人集群、网络化信息物理系统）对传统形式化方法构成巨大挑战，SCEL的操作语义不适合推理全局性、结构性和涌现性属性。

Method: 利用范畴论和层论构建SCEL的多层数学模型：组件作为拓扑空间中的点，集合作为开集，分布式知识作为层的数据；计算过程对应层的"粘合"操作；系统故障通过层上同调量化分析。

Result: 将复杂分布式系统的验证问题转化为数学对象的几何分析，系统故障可作为拓扑障碍被度量和理解。

Conclusion: 该方法为鲁棒自主系统的设计提供了深层次的结构性见解，通过几何视角为分布式系统的形式化验证开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of large-scale, decentralized systems of autonomous agents, such as swarms of robots and networked cyber-physical systems, presents a formidable challenge to traditional formal methods. The Software Component Ensemble Language (SCEL) offers a formal model for such systems, but its operational semantics is not ideal for reasoning about global, structural, and emergent properties. This report proposes a new, multi-layered mathematical model for SCEL using category theory and sheaf theory. We argue that a society of robots described in SCEL can be formally modeled as a sheaf on a topological space, where components are points, ensembles are open sets, and distributed knowledge forms the sheaf's data. In this framework, computational processes like information sharing become equivalent to the sheaf-theoretic operation of "gluing" local data. System failures can then be understood and quantified as topological obstructions, measurable by sheaf cohomology. This approach transforms the verification of a complex distributed system into the analysis of the geometry of a mathematical object, providing deep, structural insights for the design of robust autonomic systems.

</details>


### [176] [SCAN-Planner: Spatial Collision-Aware Local Planning for Route-Guided Long-Range Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19555)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yiwen Fu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAN-Planner是一种面向四足机器人的空间碰撞感知局部规划框架，采用偏航感知双圆柱足迹模型、投影A*搜索和机器人中心滑动地图，实现了在狭窄通道、杂乱室内和3D非结构化环境中的安全、平滑、高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部规划器通常使用各向同性的几何膨胀或依赖平面/高程地图表示，导致在狭窄空间中运动保守，且对悬空结构的推理能力有限。

Method: 1) 偏航感知双圆柱足迹模型建模细长机器人身体，通过在膨胀的3D占用地图中进行稀疏查询实现全身碰撞评估；2) 投影A*搜索在插值的地面跟随表面上生成无碰撞引导，通过z梯度抑制实现水平避障同时保持垂直稳定性；3) 机器人中心滑动地图与边界回退机制，提供高分辨率局部碰撞检查和局部死胡同恢复。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，SCAN-Planner在密集杂波、3D非结构化场景、楼梯穿越和长距离导航任务中生成安全、平滑且高效的轨迹。

Conclusion: SCAN-Planner通过空间碰撞感知建模有效提升了四足机器人在复杂环境中的导航能力，克服了传统方法的保守性和对悬空结构推理不足的局限。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to navigate through narrow passages, cluttered indoor scenes, and large-scale 3D unstructured environments. Existing local planners commonly approximate the robot using isotropic geometric inflation or rely on planar and elevation-map representations, leading to conservative motion in tight spaces and limited reasoning about overhanging structures. This letter presents SCAN-Planner, a spatial collision-aware local planning framework for long-range quadruped navigation. A yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint is used to model the elongated robot body, enabling whole-body collision evaluation through sparse queries in an inflated 3D occupancy map. We further introduce a projected A* search that generates collision-free guidance on an interpolated ground-following surface, with z-gradient suppression to avoid obstacles horizontally while maintaining vertical stability. For large-scale deployment, a robot-centric sliding map with boundary fallback provides high-resolution local collision checking and recovery from local dead ends. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCAN-Planner generates safe, smooth, and efficient trajectories in dense clutter, 3D unstructured scenes, stair traversal, and long-range navigation tasks.

</details>


### [177] [One Demo is Worth a Thousand Trajectories: Action-View Augmentation for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19586)
*Chuer Pan,Litian Liang,Dominik Bauer,Eric Cousineau,Benjamin Burchfiel,Siyuan Feng,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对鱼眼相机眼内手操作策略的数据增强框架，通过3D高斯溅射重建场景并优化轨迹，生成视觉真实的图像序列和物理可行的动作轨迹，有效提升机器人在相同场景和含障碍物场景中的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉运动策略对初始配置和未见障碍物敏感，易出现分布外观测导致执行失败，而大规模数据收集成本高昂，因此需要高效的数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 提出适配广角鱼眼相机的新型高斯溅射公式，用于3D场景重建与编辑（添加未见物体）；2) 利用轨迹优化生成平滑、无碰撞、利于视图渲染的动作轨迹；3) 从新视角渲染视觉观测，生成图像序列与对应动作轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，所提数据增强框架在相同场景和含障碍物的增强场景中，均显著提升了多种操作任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 该数据增强框架能有效缓解分布外观测问题，提升策略泛化能力，尤其在需要避障的场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for manipulation have demonstrated remarkable potential in modeling complex robotic behaviors, yet minor alterations in the robot's initial configuration and unseen obstacles easily lead to out-of-distribution observations. Without extensive data collection effort, these result in catastrophic execution failures. In this work, we introduce an effective data augmentation framework that generates visually realistic fisheye image sequences and corresponding physically feasible action trajectories from real-world eye-in-hand demonstrations, captured with a portable parallel gripper with a single fisheye camera. We introduce a novel Gaussian Splatting formulation, adapted to wide FoV fisheye cameras, to reconstruct and edit the 3D scene with unseen objects. We utilize trajectory optimization to generate smooth, collision-free, view-rendering-friendly action trajectories and render visual observations from corresponding novel views. Comprehensive experiments in simulation and the real world show that our augmentation framework improves the success rate for various manipulation tasks in both the same scene and the augmented scene with obstacles requiring collision avoidance.

</details>


### [178] [Fail-RAG : A Retrieval Augmented Generation Informed Framework for Robot Failure Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19598)
*Ameya Salvi,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fail-RAG框架，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的机器人操作故障检测方法，通过嵌入故障图像和上下文信息并与故障数据库进行相似度查询，结合视觉语言模型（VLM）进行故障分析，在仓库自动化场景中相比现成VLM提升25个百分点检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库自动化中机器人需要应对动态环境和任务中不可预见的意外事件（故障），而基于规则的检测方法难以适应故障形式的动态变化。

Method: 提出Fail-RAG框架：将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入并转换为向量，与故障数据库进行相似度查询以检索相关故障案例，再利用视觉语言模型（VLM）按照指令模板对故障进行详细分析。

Result: 在固定机器人臂和移动操作机上对仓库自动化常见任务进行仿真和物理实验，Fail-RAG在五种机器人操作类型的故障检测准确率上平均比现成VLM高出25个百分点。

Conclusion: Fail-RAG在真实世界故障检测中表现出有效性，为动态环境下的机器人故障检测提供了可行的RAG+ VLM解决方案。

Abstract: Industry automation is witnessing an evolution in robotics driven by both technological breakthroughs and societal changes: progress towards generalist robots, embodied and physical artificial intelligence (AI), and increasing labor shortage in manufacturing.An intelligent autonomous robot needs to not only act according to planned motions but also react to any unexpected events. In this study, we focus on such unexpected events in warehouses where robots are used for material handling. Specifically, we refer to any unexpected events as failures and develop methods to detect robot operations related failures. Rule-based detection methods may break since the form of failures could change due to the dynamic nature of both environments and tasks. We propose 'Fail-RAG', a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)-based failure detection framework where failure images and context information are embedded and queried against a failure database by calculating their similarities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are further used to analyze failures and provide details by following our instruction template. We evaluated the performance of Fail-RAG by conducting both simulation and physical experiments using fixed robot arms and a mobile manipulator for multiple tasks that are common in warehouse automation. Fail-RAG achieved 25 percentage point higher failure detection accuracy on average across five types of robot operations compared to using off-the-shelf VLMs, indicating its effectiveness for real-world failure detection.

</details>


### [179] [Formal Verification of Learned Multi-Agent Communication Policies via Decision Tree Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19632)
*Ahmad Farooq,Kamran Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于多智能体强化学习通信策略安全验证的端到端框架，通过策略抽象（蒸馏为决策树）进行形式化验证，在无人机集群任务中实现88.9%属性满足率，安全阈值全部达标，验证结果以≤0.6个百分点偏差迁移至原始神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在无人机集群、自动驾驶车队等安全关键型机器人部署中缺乏形式化安全保证，现有神经策略无法提供可证明的安全保障。

Method: 四阶段流水线：1）领域特定特征提取；2）决策树蒸馏（97.9%±1.2%保真度）；3）自动翻译为PRISM概率模型检测器规范；4）通过成对分解与联合界聚合进行PCTL属性的组合验证。

Result: 在5-7智能体多无人机协调任务中验证18个时态逻辑属性，88.9%满足率，5个安全阈值全部通过（碰撞概率0.3% vs 阈值1%）；蒙特卡洛验证显示原始神经策略偏差≤0.6个百分点；离散VQ-VIB方法保真度优势+11.6至+13.6个百分点，验证速度提升3-4倍。

Conclusion: 该框架为深度MARL与形式化安全工作流之间提供了实用的桥梁，能够对蒸馏策略抽象进行经验验证的安全验证，支持多机器人安全部署。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables agents to develop coordination strategies through emergent communication, but neural policies lack the formal safety guarantees required for safety-critical robotic deployment in drone swarms and autonomous vehicle fleets. We present the first end-to-end framework for safety verification of learned multi-agent communication policies through policy abstraction: neural policies are distilled into interpretable decision trees, then formally verified, with empirical validation confirming that verified safety properties transfer to original networks. Our four-stage pipeline consists of domain-specific feature extraction from agent observations, decision tree distillation achieving 97.9% +/- 1.2% fidelity to neural policies, automated translation to PRISM probabilistic model checker specifications with complete feature-to-state-variable correspondence, and compositional verification of Probabilistic Computation Tree Logic (PCTL) properties via pairwise decomposition with union-bound aggregation and empirical neighbor modeling. Evaluating Vector-Quantized Variational Information Bottleneck (VQ-VIB) policies for multi-drone coordination with 5-7 agents, we verify 18 temporal logic properties across safety, liveness, and cooperation, achieving 88.9% property satisfaction with all five safety thresholds satisfied (0.3% collision probability vs. 1% threshold). Monte Carlo validation of original neural policies confirms that verified safety properties transfer with <=0.6 percentage-point deviation (95% CI). Discrete VQ-VIB messages provide +11.6 to +13.6 percentage-point fidelity advantages over continuous methods, enabling 3-4x faster verification. Our framework provides empirically validated safety verification for distilled policy abstractions, serving as a practical bridge between deep MARL and formal safety workflows for multi-robot deployment.

</details>


### [180] [CTS-MoE: Implicit Terrain Adaptation via Mixture-of-Experts for Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19633)
*Francisco Affonso,Matheus P. Angarola,Ana Luiza Mineiro,Aditya Potnis,Marcelo Becker,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CTS-MoE框架，通过密集混合专家actor和基于感知的门控机制，结合多critic和任务特定价值头，解决多任务强化学习中腿式机器人在非连续地形上的共享与分离矛盾，在仿真和硬件上均优于 monolithic 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非连续地形（如楼梯、缝隙、障碍物）上的感知腿式运动需要自适应行为，单一保守步态无法应对突变的拓扑结构。多任务强化学习中的共享与分离存在矛盾：任务共享底层运动但奖励冲突，现有方法要么 monolithic 策略牺牲专门化，要么分层子策略牺牲泛化能力。

Method: 提出CTS-MoE，包含：(1) 基于密集混合专家actor和感知门控机制组合共享行为；(2) 多critic与任务特定价值头防止干扰；(3) 单阶段并行的师生训练框架处理部分可观测性，避免顺序蒸馏；(4) 部署时仅依赖感知进行路由，无需高层选择器或地形分类器。

Result: 在Unitree Go1仿真和硬件上，在可见和不可见地形上均实现任务感知专门化，相比monolithic基线，跟踪误差更低、成功率更高。

Conclusion: CTS-MoE通过密集MoE与多critic的端到端训练有效解决了多任务腿式运动中的共享-分离困境，实现了地形自适应运动，且部署时无需显式地形分类。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion over discontinuous terrain (e.g., stairs, gaps, and obstacles) requires adaptive behavior, as a single conservative gait cannot produce the anticipatory maneuvers needed for abrupt topology changes. Cast as multi-task reinforcement learning, this problem introduces a tension between sharing and separation. Tasks use a common locomotion base but have conflicting rewards, so a policy must share behavior while avoiding value interference. Prior work addresses only one side, with monolithic policies sacrificing specialization and hierarchical sub-policies sacrificing generalization across transitions and unseen terrain. We propose CTS-MoE, which combines a dense mixture-of-experts actor with perception-based gating to compose shared behaviors and a multi-critic with task-specific value heads to prevent interference. The model is trained end-to-end in a single-stage concurrent teacher-student setup that handles partial observability and avoids sequential distillation, with task labels used only during training. At deployment, routing depends solely on perception, allowing terrain adaptation without a high-level selector or terrain classifier.
