The method is anchor-less, fully decentralised, and, unlike most existing approaches, does not require controlling the robots motion to ensure team observability. It relies only on local odometry, sparse inter-agent ranging measurements, and short-range communication, all of which are widely available in practice. The algorithm adopts a multi-hypothesis Bayesian framework that maintains the entire set of feasible solutions, ensuring robustness under transient unobservable conditions. Moreover, through information sharing, each agent benefits from the estimates of the entire group, even in partially connected conditions.

</details>


### [219] [CoLI: A Reproducible Platform for Continuum Robot Learning via Monolithic 3D Printing and Isomorphic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20389)
*Ziyuan Tang,Chenxi Xiao**

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种开源连续体机器人平台，通过多材料3D打印实现单块柔性结构制造，采用同构遥操作控制（无需运动学建模），并支持基于模仿学习的自主控制，旨在解决可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因高自由度、柔顺结构和操作安全性而具有潜力，但复杂的制造装配过程、运动学建模困难以及缺乏直观控制接口导致可重复性问题，阻碍了其研究与应用推广。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印技术简化制造流程，实现单块一体化柔性结构；设计同构遥操作界面，建立直接执行器级映射，避免显式运动学建模且无奇异性；在此基础上支持基于模仿学习的自主控制。

Result: 硬件特性表征和操作任务实验表明，该平台提供了可重复的、可直接用于学习的连续体机器人系统，加速了算法开发和系统性基准测试。

Conclusion: 该开源平台通过简化制造、直观控制和自主学习支持，有效解决了连续体机器人的可重复性问题，为社区提供了加速算法开发与基准测试的工具。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer strong potential for manipulation tasks due to their high degrees of freedom, compliant structures, and operational safety. However, their adoption in both research and practical applications has been hindered by reproducibility issues arising from complex fabrication and assembly processes, challenging kinematic modeling, and a lack of intuitive control interfaces. To address these challenges, we present a novel open-source continuum robot design. The platform features a simplified fabrication pipeline enabled by multi-material 3D printing, allowing the arm to be fabricated as a monolithic compliant structure with minimal assembly. Control is achieved through an isomorphic teleoperation interface that establishes a direct actuator-level mapping, eliminating the need for explicit kinematic modeling and providing a singularity-free mapping. Building on this hardware design, the platform further supports imitation-learning-based autonomous control. The proposed system is evaluated through hardware characterization and a set of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the platform provides a reproducible, learning-ready continuum robot system, accelerating algorithmic development and systematic benchmarking for the continuum robotics community.

</details>


### [220] [Agentic AutoResearch forSpace Autonomy: An Auditable, LLM-Driven Research Agent for Aerospace Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20394)
*Amit Jain,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch框架，利用大语言模型作为离线研究代理，自动为航天器控制问题开发策略，并内置可信层对每个结果进行种子噪声检测。在交会和对接两个问题上，该方法显著优于无定向搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器制导、导航与控制功能越来越多地采用从专家求解器中提取的学习策略，但开发这样的策略本身就是一个研究过程：研究者需要选择架构和超参数、运行实验，并判断改进是否真实还是仅为种子噪声。现有方法缺乏自动化验证机制。

Method: 提出AutoResearch框架，包含大语言模型作为离线研究代理和内置可信层。代理读取问题描述和运行历史，提出并执行对训练脚本的单次编辑，记录结果。可信层对每个结果进行三项检查：测量每问题种子噪声、重新种验证最佳配置、留一法剪枝代理编辑。同一循环不变地应用于两个航天控制问题。

Result: 在两个航天控制问题（Clohessy-Wiltshire相对交会和避碰对接）中，审计后的策略以多个标准差超过测量的种子噪声。无定向搜索在相同参数上无法做到。在对接问题上，无定向搜索无法产生可行策略，而学习策略在每个种子上都保持在禁飞区之外。

Conclusion: AutoResearch框架能够自主驱动航天器控制策略的研究循环，并通过可信层确保结果的可靠性，在复杂航天控制问题上显著优于无定向搜索方法。

Abstract: Spacecraft guidance, navigation, and control functions are increasingly realized as learned policies distilled from expert solvers. Developing such a policy is itself a research process: an investigator selects an architecture and hyperparameters, runs experiments, and must determine whether an apparent improvement is genuine or merely seed noise. This paper presents AutoResearch, a framework in which a large language model autonomously drives that loop for aerospace control problems, coupled with a credibility layer, built into the loop, that certifies each reported result against the problem's own measured seed noise. The language model serves only as the offline research agent that develops the control policy; the trained policy it produces is then deployed onboard the spacecraft, while the model itself never operates the vehicle. At each iteration the agent reads a plain-language problem description and the run history, proposes a single edit to the training script, executes it, and logs the outcome. No reported result is credited until it passes the same three checks: measured per-problem seed noise, reseeded verification of the best configuration, and leave-one-out pruning of the agent's edits. The same loop is applied, unchanged, to two aerospace control problems: a Clohessy-Wiltshire relative rendezvous and a safety-constrained collision-avoidance docking past a keep-out zone, each calibrated against a known optimal control benchmark. In both, the audited policy clears the measured seed noise by many standard deviations; an undirected search over the same parameters does not. On the docking problem the gap becomes categorical: undirected search yields no feasible policy, while the learned policy stays outside the keep-out zone on every seed.

</details>


### [221] [LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20424)
*Shikuan Shi,Chunran Zheng,Jiaming Xu,Tianyong Ye,Tao Yu,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIT-GS提出了一种结合LiDAR、惯性、热成像的高斯泼溅框架，通过LiDAR导出的平面几何约束，在弱纹理和光照变化场景下显著提升了几何精度和渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-惯性-视觉(LIV)高斯建图框架依赖RGB光度线索，在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下表现脆弱，亟需一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用LIV视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点建立热-LiDAR关联；2) 将加权LiDAR点到平面残差引入光束法平差，在弱热监督下联合优化相机位姿和3D点；3) 引入LiDAR平面正则化的可微泼溅目标函数，约束渲染3D点与局部观测平面对齐。

Result: 在自有序列和公开数据集上，LIT-GS在几何精度和渲染质量上持续优于最先进的LIV基高斯泼溅基线方法，尤其在挑战性光照条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: 融合LiDAR平面几何约束能够有效克服热成像低对比度带来的结构漂移和表面增厚问题，提升高斯泼溅在退化环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [222] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy是一个基于有限元法的视觉触觉传感器仿真框架，集成于Isaac Sim中，通过UIPC求解器直接计算柯西应力张量，提供物理真实的力场仿真，支持多种传感器，在60个并行环境中达555 FPS，力场范围1.2556 N至4.7332 N内SSIM超过0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的触觉传感器仿真方法无法在GPU加速的机器人平台中提供精确的机械应力场，缺乏从第一性原理出发的物理真实力计算。

Method: 基于UIPC求解器，从超弹性本构方程直接计算柯西应力张量，投影至接触面获得牵引力和压力分布；自动网格生成与几何感知自适应细化；模块化传感器接口。

Result: 单环境33.40 FPS，60并行环境555 FPS聚合吞吐量，应力提取开销低于1 ms；力范围1.2556 N至4.7332 N内SSIM超0.93。

Conclusion: TaCauchy能够为下游机器人操作任务提供精确、物理真实的力监督信号，验证了仿真与真实触觉响应之间的强一致性。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [223] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的块坐标下降框架，将自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新和最小加权协方差行列式估计器相结合，实现在离群值存在下同时估计状态和测量协方差的自调优联合估计器，无需手动调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器测量常被离群值和非高斯噪声污染，经典状态 estimators 会产生有偏估计；鲁棒估计器能抑制离群值但不估计测量协方差，而联合状态与协方差估计器假设高斯残差和固定损失形状参数，缺少一个同时处理离群值并估计协方差的统一框架。

Method: 提出Block-Coordinate Descent统一框架，包含三个核心组件：①范数感知的自适应鲁棒损失函数；②迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）状态更新；③最小加权协方差行列式（Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant）协方差估计器，三者交替优化实现自调优联合估计。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛仿真和真实超宽带（UWB）定位实验（杂波非视距环境）中，所提估计器一致恢复真实内点测量协方差，状态估计精度达到或超过所有基线方法，且无需手动参数调整。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功融合了鲁棒估计和协方差估计能力，实现了离群值环境下的自调优联合状态与协方差估计，在准确性和实用性上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [224] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-Planner接口，利用视觉语言模型从基于锚点的规划器生成的候选轨迹中选择最佳索引，并通过几何相似性与指数衰减的轨迹级融合层解决VLM延迟问题，在真实世界 sidewalk 导航中将ADE降低30%，在模拟中延迟高达5秒时仍保持>80%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型规划器在 sidewalk 导航中生成的候选轨迹集的评分函数在挑战性场景（如路口、行人相遇）中难以选出最优轨迹，导致机器人驶入草地、朝向行人或错误方向，存在轨迹评分差距（trajectory scoring gap），原因在于规划器缺乏高层场景理解能力。

Method: 提出VLM-Planner接口：使用VLM从规划器的候选轨迹集中选择索引；设计无需训练、容忍延迟的轨迹级融合层（Score Fusion），通过几何相似性（geometric similarity）与指数衰减（exponential decay）将过时的VLM选择转化为实时规划器评分。

Result: 在约2,000个真实世界挑战性场景中，VLM选择相比规划器最优选择实现30%的ADE降低；在模拟中，Score Fusion在延迟高达5秒时仍保持>80%成功率；在移动机器人实际校园 sidewalk 导航中验证了系统在可变网络延迟下的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不替换端到端VLA模型的情况下，利用VLM弥补规划器的场景理解不足，并通过延迟鲁棒的融合机制使VLM适用于实时控制循环，在保持规划器在常规场景竞争力的同时显著提升挑战性场景性能。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [225] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GroundControl，一种轨迹一致的不确定性估计方法，通过卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标动态的统计偏差来预测视觉语言导航代理的故障（如振荡、停滞、低效绕路）。在 Selective Risk–Coverage Navigation (SRCN) 协议下，该方法在五个 EB-Navigation 数据集上显著优于熵、共形和启发式基线，实现了接近 oracle 的失败排序性能（GPT-4o 模型加权平均 E-AURC_{SR}=0.0024）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航智能体在基准任务上平均成功率可观，但常因振荡、停滞、低效绕路等可预测的轨迹级故障而失败。可靠部署需要能够在执行过程中提前预警此类动态故障的不确定性信号，而非仅反映瞬时动作熵。

Method: 提出 GroundControl 方法：使用恒速卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标演化，结合归一化创新统计量与轨迹特征（进度、单调性、路径效率、振荡行为），聚合得到轨迹一致的不确定性评分。同时提出 Selective Risk–Coverage Navigation (SRCN) 评估协议，利用风险-覆盖曲线和 AURC/E-AURC 指标量化不确定性排序质量。

Result: 在五个 EB-Navigation 分割（N=300 个 episode）上，GroundControl 在基于成功率和 SPL 的选择性评估中均达到最优。GPT-4o 模型在成功率选择性风险下加权平均 E-AURC_{SR}=0.0024，大幅优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 对目标导向动态的偏差建模能够为视觉语言导航代理提供可解释且稳健的故障预警信号，显著优于基于熵和共形预测的不确定性方法。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [226] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GazeLNN，一种基于液体神经网络和MobileNetV3的轻量级扫描路径预测模型，仅需0.61 GFLOPs计算量，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上达到SOTA性能（ScanMatch 0.47），计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍，并成功集成到无人机自主导航的主动相机-机器人控制策略中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉注意力依赖结构化扫描路径高效处理场景，但现有预测模型计算成本高昂，阻碍了该行为在机器人自主系统中的实际应用。

Method: 以液体神经网络（LNN）作为循环引擎，MobileNetV3进行特征提取，采用自回归方式预测序列注视热图，条件为当前视觉刺激和注视历史。

Result: 仅需0.61 GFLOPs，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上ScanMatch得分0.47；计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍；通过强化学习训练主动相机-机器人控制策略，在无人机上实现真实世界部署验证。

Conclusion: GazeLNN证明了高效注意力建模在机器人自主系统中的实用价值，能够以极低计算开销实现人类注视引导的感知，为资源受限的自主机器人提供了可行的视觉注意力方案。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [227] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了弹性连续体机器人的运动规划，重点是多准则决策及其对路径规划算法的影响。通过将遗传算法和A*算法与层次分析法（AHP）结合，考虑距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤和精度四个准则来评估路径质量。实验在两种模拟环境中进行，结果表明遗传算法的性能时间不受环境基数影响，能生成更多样化的路径，提升机器人弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了延长连续体机器人的维护周期，需要提高其弹性（resilience）。现有研究缺乏将多准则决策融入路径规划以平衡多个性能指标（如距离、损伤、精度）的系统性实验分析。

Method: 采用遗传算法和A*算法作为基础路径规划算法，引入层次分析法（AHP）作为多准则决策工具，对生成的路径进行质量评估。AHP考虑距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤和精度四个准则。在两种不同模拟环境（单/多路径点环境与仅多路径点环境）中进行实验。

Result: 遗传算法的性能时间不依赖环境基数（路径点数量），而A*算法受其影响。遗传算法能生成更多样化的路径，从而更好地增强连续体机器人的弹性。

Conclusion: 在多准则路径规划中，遗传算法相比A*算法更适合弹性连续体机器人，因其对环境复杂度不敏感且路径多样性更高，有助于延长机器人维护周期。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [228] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动框架，利用超400万帧人类手指运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置，优化生成树结构机器人手部设计，包括6自由度通用手和低自由度任务专用手，并采用强化学习加速搜索，实物实验表明其指尖跟踪精度优于商用机器人手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制方面进步迅速，但学习机器人物理本体仍然困难，因为设计与控制的联合搜索构成了非常大的组合问题。现有方法难以高效地从人类示教数据中直接生成机器人手部结构设计。

Method: 提出数据驱动框架，利用超过400万帧人类日常操作中的指尖运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置来优化树结构机器人手部设计。设计了6自由度通用手和带空间四杆仿生关节的低自由度任务专用手。使用强化学习智能体提出手部设计和关节角度，将搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。采用一体化打印关节直接制造。

Result: 6自由度手在遥操作指尖跟踪上达到高精度，优于现有商用机器人手；3自由度专用手以更低机械复杂度复现了结构化人类和合成轨迹。强化学习将设计搜索时间从小时级降至分钟级。

Conclusion: 大规模人体运动数据不仅可用于训练机器人控制器，也可作为优化和生成机器人物理本体的参考，证明了数据驱动下从人类示教到机器人本体设计的可行性。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [229] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM提出了一种结合高效持久记忆的世界动作模型，通过混合记忆设计（近期帧、事件边界锚点帧和压缩要点令牌）和定制注意力机制，在保持计算效率的同时，在长时程、依赖记忆的机器人操控任务中优于强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）存在根本性权衡：高效推理的方法仅依赖有限窗口的近期观测，在非马尔可夫环境中表现不佳；而保持长期历史的方法其时间和空间成本随序列长度显著增长。因此需要一种既能保留长期记忆又保持计算效率的模型。

Method: MemoryWAM采用混合记忆设计，包含三部分：近期帧（提供详细短期上下文）、事件边界锚点帧（捕捉关键事件）以及压缩要点令牌（gist tokens，总结长期历史）。配合定制的注意力机制，实现对详细短期上下文和压缩长期上下文的高效检索，降低了推理延迟和GPU内存使用。

Result: 在长时程、依赖记忆的机器人操控任务中（包括仿真和真实世界），MemoryWAM在性能上优于强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基线和WAM基线，同时保持了良好的计算效率。

Conclusion: MemoryWAM通过高效的持久记忆机制成功解决了WAM模型中长期依赖与计算效率之间的权衡问题，为现实世界中需要记忆和动态建模的鲁棒机器人操控提供了有前景的范式。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [230] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HUG，一个基于流匹配模型的人手抓取生成方法，利用第一人称RGB-D图像生成多样化的人类抓取姿态，并可将抓取重定向到多种机器人手，实现零样本日常场景抓取。同时构建了包含100万帧的第一人称抓取数据集1M-HUGs和评估基准HUG-Bench，在真实世界实验中超越了现有方法23%和34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人抓取能力远不及人类，而人类每天抓取数千个物体，是最自然的机器人抓取数据来源。作者旨在利用人类抓取数据来提升机器人抓取的通用性。

Method: 使用智能眼镜收集第一人称RGB-D图像数据集1M-HUGs（100万帧、27.8小时、6707个物体实例）。提出基于流匹配（flow-matching）的生成模型，融合RGB和深度观测，输出由手腕平移、手腕旋转和MANO手部姿态参数化的抓取。预测的抓取可重定向到多种机器人手，实现零样本抓取。

Result: 在HUG-Bench的90个未见物体（5种几何类别）上评估，HUG在真实世界30个物体测试集上分别超过最先进的抓取基线23%和34%。

Conclusion: HUG通过大规模人类抓取数据学习和流匹配生成模型，实现了从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化、可重定向到机器人的自然抓取，在真实场景中显著优于现有方法，为机器人抓取提供了一种从人类示范学习的新范式。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Weight Adaptation for Improving Parallel Performance of Adaptive Stochastic Natural Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19861)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-ASNG算法，通过权重自适应机制改进ASNG，在二元优化问题中显著优于PBIL和ASNG，尤其在强噪声和大种群规模场景下表现高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率模型进化算法（如ASNG）虽能自适应学习率，但权重参数作为常见超参数，在大规模并行评估场景下缺乏有效的自适应设置方法，限制了算法性能。

Method: WA-ASNG在ASNG基础上引入权重自适应机制：通过累积自然梯度估计更新方向的信号，然后利用梯度上升法最大化该信号，从而自适应更新权重参数。学习率自适应保证期望目标值单调改进的充分条件，权重自适应则旨在最大化这一改进。

Result: 在二元优化问题中，种群规模25-100的设置下，WA-ASNG优于PBIL和ASNG；且在强噪声环境下仍能保持高效性能。

Conclusion: 权重自适应机制能有效提升概率模型进化算法在大种群规模下的优化性能与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms are promising for black-box optimization. Specifically, the adaptive stochastic natural gradient (ASNG) adaptively updates its learning rate, a typical hyperparameter in probabilistic model-based evolutionary algorithms, thereby realizing efficient and robust optimization. Although weight parameters are common hyperparameters, with the increasing demand for parallel evaluation of time-consuming tasks, it remains unclear how to set suitable weights for larger population sizes. In this paper, we propose Weight Adaptation ASNG (WA-ASNG), which incorporates a weight adaptation mechanism into ASNG. We calculated the estimated signal of the update direction from the accumulations of the natural gradient. Then, to maximize the signal, WA-ASNG adaptively updates its weight parameters by a gradient ascent over the optimization. While the learning rate adaptation plays a role in satisfying a sufficient condition for monotonic improvement of the expected objective value, the mechanism of weight adaptation is intended to maximize this improvement. The experimental results demonstrate that WA-ASNG outperforms PBIL and ASNG across various settings with population sizes ranging from 25 to 100 for binary optimization problems. Furthermore, WA-ASNG can perform efficiently in the presence of strong noise. Our code is available at https://github.com/shiralab/WA-ASNG .

</details>


### [232] [Hybrid ANN-SNN Pipeline with Local Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20151)
*Denis Larionov,Khairutin Shtanchaev,Mikhail Kiselev,Mikhail Korovin,Ivan Tugoy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种混合ANN-SNN流水线，将预训练人工神经网络（EfficientNet编码器）与脉冲神经网络分类器（CoLaNET）结合，通过率编码转换激活值为脉冲序列，并利用局部生物启发学习规则训练SNN分类器，在64类ImageNet上达到99.09%准确率，性能媲美传统深度网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用预训练ANN的丰富嵌入来提升SNN性能，同时避免端到端梯度传播，实现生物合理性与高效性的统一。

Method: 混合ANN-SNN流水线：预训练EfficientNet编码器 + CoLaNET脉冲分类器；通过率编码将编码器激活值转换为脉冲序列；使用局部生物启发学习规则训练SNN分类器。

Result: 在64类ImageNet基准测试上达到99.09%准确率，性能与传统深度网络相当。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种生物合理且高效的框架，能够将强大的预训练编码器适配到下游SNN任务中。

Abstract: This work proposes a hybrid ANN-SNN pipeline that effectively leverages the rich embeddings of pretrained artificial neural networks (ANNs) to enable high-performance spiking neural networks (SNNs). The architecture couples a pretrained EfficientNet encoder with a CoLaNET spiking classifier. We convert the encoder's activations into spike trains via rate-coding and train the subsequent SNN classifier using local, biologically inspired learning rules, bypassing end-to-end gradient propagation. This approach achieves 99.09% accuracy on a 64-class ImageNet benchmark, demonstrating performance on par with conventional deep networks. The work presents a biologically plausible and efficient framework for adapting powerful pretrained encoders to downstream spiking neural network tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Supporting Design Decisions in Rule-Based Model Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19342)
*Dejan Stojimirovic,Sinisa Neskovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在基于规则的模型变换中显式建模和管理设计决策的方法，通过决策模型、绑定模型和配置模型将设计决策与变换实现分离，并建立形式化数学框架，在嵌入式领域特定语言中实现了可变性支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型变换中，设计决策（如何将源元素映射到目标工件）通常嵌入在变换源码中，导致灵活性、可重用性和可追溯性受限。

Method: 提出三种模型机制：1) 决策模型独立于源建模语言捕获设计决策及其选项；2) 绑定模型将决策关联到特定源元模型概念；3) 配置模型记录每个源元素的具体选项选择。变换规则中的可变性点在执行时动态解析，并通过追溯模型记录应用过程。同时建立了形式化数学框架定义核心概念并证明关键性质。

Result: 在四个相互关联的工件模型组成的实用架构中实现了形式化概念，并通过扩展嵌入式领域特定语言（DSL）以及完整的ER到关系模型变换示例验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功将设计决策显式化并与变换实现分离，提升了变换的灵活性、可重用性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Model Driven Engineering relies on model transformations to automate the derivation of target models or source code from source models. However, the design decisions that govern how source elements are mapped to target artifacts typically remain embedded in the transformation source code, limiting flexibility, reusability, and traceability. This paper proposes an approach to explicitly model and manage design decisions in rule-based model transformations. Design decisions are separated from transformation implementation through three mechanisms. First, a decision model captures design decisions and their options independently of any source modeling language for a given transformation domain. Second, a binding model connects these decisions to specific metamodel concepts in a given source modeling language, enabling reuse of the same design knowledge across transformations from similar languages. Third, a configuration model records the specific option chosen for each applicable source model element, with defaults pre-selected automatically. During execution, variability points in transformation rules are resolved dynamically according to configured choices. A trace model records which rules and options were applied to produce each target element. We establish a formal mathematical framework that defines the core concepts of variability-based transformations and proves key properties of the resulting transformations. The formal concepts are realized in a practical architecture of four interconnected artifact models. We demonstrate practical feasibility by extending an existing embedded domain-specific language for model-driven engineering with variability support and illustrate the approach with a complete ER-to-Relational transformation example.

</details>


### [234] [AgentArmor: A Framework, Evaluation, \& Mitigation of Coding Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19380)
*Kenneth Ge,Andre Assis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI编码代理在软件工程和部署中出现的三种失败机制（欠规范、能力错误、代理框架错误），提出了AgentArmor代理框架修改方案，通过扩展系统提示、命令分类器、“三振出局”策略、确定性护栏和上下文编辑工具，显著提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的广泛采用暴露出罕见但破坏性极强的失败模式，需要系统性理解和缓解这些安全风险。

Method: 基于8种受真实部署失败启发的评估（共20个编码环境和59个合成对话模板），识别出三种失败机制（欠规范、能力错误、代理框架错误），并提出AgentArmor框架修改方案，包含扩展系统提示、独立命令分类器、“三振出局”策略、确定性护栏和上下文编辑工具。

Result: AgentArmor在统计显著数量的样本上实现了更高的安全性。

Conclusion: 为当前编码代理提出了具体的缓解措施，并为未来代理框架功能设计提供了设计哲学。

Abstract: Software engineering and deployment are increasingly being delegated to AI coding agents. The scale of their adoption is surfacing rare, but highly destructive, failure modes. In this paper, we study these failure modes as stemming from three distinct mechanisms: underspecification, where default model behavior is unsafe; capability errors, where the safe action is available but the model does not adhere to it due to bias or capability limitations; and agent harness errors, where the model fails to execute the safe action through the harness. We evaluate these across 8 different evaluations, each inspired by real-life deployment failures, totaling 20 coding environments and 59 synthetic transcript templates. Based on this evaluation, we propose AgentArmor, an agent harness modification, to mitigate these errors. By adding an extended system prompt, a separate command classifier, a ``3 strikes'' policy, deterministic guardrails, and tools for the agent to edit its own context, we show that AgentArmor is safer across a statistically significant number of samples. Thus, we suggest concrete mitigations for current coding agents and a design philosophy for future agent harness features.

</details>


### [235] [OpenRath: Session-Centered Runtime State for Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19409)
*Fukang Wen,Zhijie Wang,Ruilin Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenRath提出了一种以Session为核心第一类运行时抽象的多智能体系统编程模型，通过将运行时状态（会话、工具效果、内存事件等）封装在可分支、可检查、可重放的Session值中，解决了智能体系统运行时状态碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统中，运行时状态（如对话记录、工具执行效果、内存事件、工作空间布局、分支来源和重放证据）分散记录在不同位置，导致难以检查或复现。

Method: 借鉴PyTorch编程模型的设计理念，提出Session作为核心第一类运行时抽象。Session是可分支、可检查、可重放、可感知后端且可组合的值，它记录对话片段、沙箱位置、血统元数据、令牌使用、待处理工作和工具证据，并定义了内存交互进入运行时记录的位置。系统还定义了Sandbox、Tool、Agent、Memory、Workflow和Selector等组件，其中Selector将控制流转化为运行时路由决策。

Result: 提出了完整的编程模型、架构和审计里程碑，并定义了证据协议。分支、合并和重放成为显式的运行时操作，而非从外部轨迹重建的状态。

Conclusion: Session为智能体系统提供了可审计组合的第一类运行时值。广泛的定量比较、实时提供商质量、可选后端可用性和内存质量留待后续评估。

Abstract: Modern agent systems often suffer from fragmented runtime state: transcripts, tool effects, memory events, workspace placement, branch provenance, and replay evidence are recorded separately and become difficult to inspect or reproduce. OpenRath addresses this issue with a PyTorch-like programming model for multi-agent, multi-session systems. The analogy concerns the role of a central first-class runtime abstraction, not tensor computation. Its core abstraction is Session, the runtime value passed between agents and workflows. A Session is branchable, inspectable, replayable, backend-aware, and composable. It records conversation chunks, sandbox placement, lineage metadata, token usage, pending work, and tool evidence, while defining where memory interactions enter the runtime record. Since this state is carried by the same value used in program execution, fork, merge, and replay become explicit runtime operations rather than states reconstructed from external traces. OpenRath further defines Sandbox, Tool, Agent, Memory, Workflow, and Selector, with Selector turning control flow into runtime-routed decisions. This report presents the programming model, architecture, audited milestones, and evidence protocol. Its claims are limited to controlled runtime properties, while broad quantitative comparisons, live-provider quality, optional-backend availability, and memory quality are left for follow-on evaluation. The central thesis is that Session provides agent systems with a first-class runtime value for auditable composition.

</details>


### [236] [DynAMO:Dynamic Asset Management Orchestration via Topological Multi-Agent Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19382)
*Kanishk Kushwaha,Vikrant Vinod Bansode,Harsh Vardhan,Dhaval C. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DynAMO是一个基于Plan-then-Execute架构的工业资产管理引擎，通过顺序工作流和并行工作流实现任务编排。在AssetOpsBench基准测试中，并行执行将端到端延迟中位数降低1.6倍（高度并行任务达1.8倍），结构化上下文剪枝减少约30%推理延迟，并展现出良好的鲁棒性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体在工业资产生命周期中提供端到端自动化，但在实际工业4.0部署中受限于延迟、并发不稳定性以及安全风险。

Method: 提出DynAMO引擎，采用Plan-then-Execute架构生成可验证的工作流图，支持SequentialWorkflow（拓扑执行）和ParallelWorkflow（依赖感知并发），通过动态识别独立任务在保证结构正确性和安全性的同时提升效率。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench上六项实验表明：并行执行降低端到端延迟中位数1.6倍（高度并行任务1.8倍）；LLM推理与编排占总执行时间90%以上，识别出模型推理是主要瓶颈；结构化上下文剪枝降低约30%推理延迟；系统在受控故障注入下保持优雅降级；可重复性分析证实执行稳定，并行调度降低延迟方差。

Conclusion: DynAMO为工业4.0自动化流水线中可扩展、安全且延迟感知的智能体部署提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: While LLM-powered agents offer end-to-end automation for industrial asset lifecycles, real-world Industry 4.0 deployment is hindered by latency, concurrency instability, and safety risks. We present DynAMO (Dynamic Asset Management Orchestration), a deployment-ready engine using a Plan-then-Execute architecture to generate verifiable workflow graphs. DynAMO supports both SequentialWorkflow (topological execution) and ParallelWorkflow (dependency-aware concurrency). By dynamically identifying independent tasks, DynAMO preserves structural correctness and safety while significantly improving efficiency through controlled reasoning overlap.
  Across six controlled experiments on the AssetOpsBench industrial benchmark, DynAMO demonstrates substantial performance and robustness gains. Parallel execution reduces end-to-end latency by a median of 1.6x over sequential orchestration, rising to 1.8x on highly parallelizable workflows. After instrumenting external tool calls with realistic latencies, a latency decomposition shows that LLM reasoning and orchestration still account for more than 90% of execution time, identifying model inference as the primary system bottleneck. Structured context pruning reduces inference latency by approximately 30%, and DynAMO maintains correct functional behaviour (task completion, agent sequencing, and output quality) while exhibiting graceful degradation under controlled fault injection. Reproducibility analysis further confirms stable execution under repeated runs, with parallel scheduling reducing latency variance. These findings establish DynAMO as a practical blueprint for scalable, safe, and latency-aware agent deployment in Industry 4.0 automation pipelines. Code is available at: https://github.com/kushwaha001/DynAMO

</details>


### [237] [Bistable by Construction: Wall-Clock-Calibrated State Monitors Have No Moment-Detection Regime at Agent Cadence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19386)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了对先前发表的"状态饱和陷阱"的修正：该陷阱并非源于情感引擎本身，而是由于积分器在智能体流上使用壁钟时间校准（而非样本时间校准）所致。壁钟时间积分器在dt≤1s时恒定报警，dt≥60s时完全静默，临界区间在(1,30]s；实际智能体运行延迟中位1.53s恰好落入陷阱区间，而样本时间CUSUM完全不受dt影响。壁钟泄漏积分器无法作为智能体流的瞬态检测器，而上升沿触发器可逃脱陷阱但无法恢复人工干预时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正先前关于"状态饱和陷阱"的发现：该陷阱原被归因于连续情感引擎在SWE-bench调试智能体上导致近恒定报警，但后续审计发现引擎在动作间接收dt=0，指数衰减从未生效，实际是纯累加器结果。本文以此缺陷作为实验，探究监控器动力学是应基于样本时间还是壁钟时间校准这一关键变量。

Method: 对20条轨迹进行预注册的均匀间隔dt∈{0..600}s扫描实验，对比壁钟时间水平触发器、最小壁钟累加器、样本时间CUSUM和上升沿触发器四种监控设计在不同dt下的表现，并测量真实智能体运行中的动作间延迟分布。

Result: 壁钟时间水平触发器存在双稳态：dt≤1s时20/20恒定报警（中位18次触发），dt≥60s时完全静默，所有临界dt在(1,30]s区间；真实智能体延迟中位1.53s（p90 2.33s）落入陷阱区间。样本时间CUSUM在所有dt下20/20完全不受影响。最小壁钟累加器复现相同悬崖。上升沿触发器在每种条件下仅触发0-3次。

Conclusion: 壁钟时间校准的泄漏积分器监控器在任何条件下都无法作为智能体流的瞬态检测器；上升沿触发器虽能逃脱陷阱，但无法恢复人工干预的时序精度。监控器动力学的校准时间（样本时间vs壁钟时间）是比引擎本身更关键的结构性属性。

Abstract: Runtime monitors for autonomous agents commonly threshold an accumulated internal state - a behavioural baseline, a drift statistic, or, in our prior work, a modelled affective state. We previously reported a State Saturation Trap: threshold-on-state triggers over a continuous affect engine become near-constant alarms on SWE-bench debugging agents (Modgil 2026). A post-release audit found the engine received dt=0 between actions, so its exponential decay never operated: the published trap is a pure-accumulator result. We correct the record (erratum, v2) and treat the flaw as an experiment. The key variable it exposes is whether a monitor's dynamics are calibrated in sample time (per observation, as in CUSUM) or wall-clock time (half-lives in seconds, as in affect models and EMA baselines). On fixed-rate streams these coincide; on agent streams, where inter-action time varies by orders of magnitude, they do not. A pre-registered sweep over uniform intervals (dt in {0..600}s) on 20 trajectories shows the wall-clock level trigger has two regimes: at dt<=1s a constant alarm (20/20; median 18 firings); at dt>=60s silent. Every critical dt lies in (1,30]s. Real agent runs measure latency at median 1.53s (p90 2.33s); real coding cadence sits inside the trap regime, vindicating the empirical finding under a corrected mechanism. The structure is a property of the calibration class, not the engine: a minimal wall-clock accumulator over the raw error stream reproduces the same cliff, while a sample-time CUSUM over the identical stream is exactly dt-invariant (20/20). A rising-edge trigger with hysteresis fires 0-3 times per trajectory in every condition. We conclude that wall-clock-calibrated leaky-integrator monitors admit no regime in which they act as moment detectors on agent streams; transition detection escapes the trap at every cadence, but does not recover human intervention timing.

</details>


### [238] [Interpretable and Verifiable Hardware Generation with LLM-Driven Stepwise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19387)
*You Li,Samuel Mandell,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LLM创造力与形式化方法严谨性的硬件生成框架，通过迭代应用变换规则，将设计规范转化为正确性有保障的RTL程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程中表现优异，但在RTL生成中存在幻觉问题（引入语义和逻辑错误），硬件工程师因芯片设计制造的高风险性而不愿依赖LLM。

Method: 设计一套覆盖多种设计决策和硬件特征的变换规则，LLM代理通过迭代应用这些规则，将设计规范逐步转换为RTL程序。

Result: 实验证明了该框架的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 结合LLM的创造力与形式化方法的数学严谨性及可解释性，能够生成正确性有保障的RTL代码，克服LLM在硬件生成中的幻觉问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in software development. However, they are susceptible to hallucinations, meaning that they can introduce subtle semantic and logical errors. Due to the high stakes in chip design and manufacturing, hardware engineers are still reluctant to rely on LLMs for register-transfer level (RTL) generation. In this paper, we propose a hardware generation framework that combines the creativity and broad knowledge of LLMs with the explainability and mathematical rigor of formal methods. Specifically, we devise a set of transformation rules that cover various design decisions and hardware features. By iteratively applying these rules, an LLM agent can convert a design specification into an RTL program with guaranteed correctness. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the framework.

</details>


### [239] [Beyond the GUI Paradigm: Do Mobile Agents Need the Phone Screen?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19388)
*Li Gu,Zihuan Jiang,Linqiang Guo,Zhixiang Chi,Ziqiang Wang,Huan Liu,Yuanhao Yu,Tse-Hsun Chen,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证移动端CLI代理应作为GUI的同等范式，通过实验证明纯文本CLI代理（Claude Code达71.8%/51.9%）在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld上胜过所有可复现的GUI基线，并引入CLI-Advantage任务套件展示其在批量操作、多条件过滤等场景的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端代理研究过度集中于GUI范式，忽略了移动平台同样存在CLI接口，而CLI能直接访问设备服务与数据，具有独特优势。

Method: 评估三种编码代理（Claude Code, Terminus-2, mini-swe-agent）在四种模型API上的表现，与三种可复现GUI基线在AndroidWorld和MobileWorld基准上对比；构建CLI-Advantage任务套件（45个模板，5类场景）；提供oracle CLI解决方案作为上界。

Result: Claude Code (Opus 4.7) 达71.8%（AndroidWorld）和51.9%（MobileWorld），超越所有GUI基线；Oracle CLI上界为88.8%和86.3%；CLI代理在全部5类CLI-Advantage任务中完胜GUI基线，步骤数更少（10.7 vs 18.6）。

Conclusion: CLI范式在移动端代理中具有显著优势，应作为GUI的同等方案受到重视；当前CLI代理与oracle上界仍有差距，未来有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in mobile agents are dominated by the GUI paradigm, in which agents perceive UI information and emit screen interactions. However, mobile platforms also expose a command-line interface (CLI) that provides direct access to device services and data. We argue CLI deserves first-class consideration alongside GUI. We evaluate three coding agents (Claude Code, Terminus-2, mini-swe-agent) across four model APIs on AndroidWorld and MobileWorld without any mobile-specific post-training, comparing against three reproducible GUI baselines (GUI-Owl-1.5-32B, MAI-UI, Qwen3-VL-32B). Claude Code (Opus 4.7) reaches 71.8\% and 51.9\%, outperforming every reproducible GUI baseline (69.3/68.1/57.8\% on AndroidWorld; 43.2/26.3/13.3\% on MobileWorld), while every other CLI configuration remains competitive. To establish the paradigm's ceiling, we provide oracle CLI solutions that reach 88.8\% on AndroidWorld (103/116 tasks CLI-solvable) and 86.3\% on MobileWorld (101/117 tasks CLI-solvable), indicating substantial room for future improvement. To cover everyday user intents beyond the GUI scope, we introduce the \textbf{CLI-Advantage Task Suite}, comprising 45 templates across five categories: bulk operations, multi-condition filtering, aggregation, cross-app workflows, and hidden device state. Every CLI agent outperforms every GUI baseline in all five categories, with substantially fewer steps per task (10.7 vs.\ 18.6). To support future research on mobile CLI agents, we will open-source agent implementations, oracle solutions, the CLI-Advantage suite, and evaluation infrastructure.

</details>


### [240] [Execution-bound advisory automation for agentic AI: a reproducible AIBOM-driven CSAF-VEX framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19390)
*Petar Radanliev,Omar Santos,Carsten Maple,Kay Atefi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个协议驱动框架，将SBOM和AIBOM与确定性环境捕获及运行时遥测绑定，通过静态和运行时证据计算可利用性，生成CSAF VEX公告，并在合成Agentic AI工作负载（50-5000组件）上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM/AIBOM缺乏与运行时环境的绑定，且漏洞可利用性评估依赖纯静态分析，无法反映实际激活条件，导致安全公告准确性不足。

Method: 1) 将SBOM/AIBOM与确定性环境捕获及结构化运行时遥测绑定；2) 基于声明组件、观测到的激活条件和强制执行策略计算可利用性；3) 结合静态和运行时证据生成CSAF VEX公告，经加密签名并通过确定性回放验证。

Result: 在合成Agentic AI工作负载（约10000组件条目，规模50-5000组件）上进行了评估，整合了OSV、GitHub Advisory、KEV和EPSS数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架通过运行时证据增强漏洞可利用性评估，并实现可验证的加密签名安全公告生成。

Abstract: A protocol driven framework is presented that binds SBOM and AIBOM artefacts to deterministic environment capture and structured runtime telemetry. Exploitability is computed from declared artefacts, observed activation conditions, and enforced execution policies. CSAF VEX advisories are generated from combined static and runtime evidence, cryptographically signed, and validated through deterministic replay. Evaluation uses approximately 10000 component entries across synthetic Agentic AI workloads 50 to 5000 components, incorporating OSV, GitHub Advisory, KEV, and EPSS datasets.

</details>


### [241] [DevOps and General Developers: Insights from Stack Overflow's 2023 Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19395)
*Hasan Abdulla,Fatema AlJazeeri,Fawzi AlBalooshi,Jaflah Al-Ammary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于2023年Stack Overflow开发者调查数据，采用定量分析方法比较了DevOps专家和普通软件开发者在工具使用、技术偏好、人口统计特征和职业经验方面的差异。研究发现两类群体在工具和技术偏好上没有显著差异，都使用Docker和Kubernetes等工具，但普通开发者工具使用范围更广；人口统计上普通开发者更年轻，DevOps专业人员处于职业中期；两类群体都适应远程和混合工作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对DevOps专家和普通软件开发者角色差异的系统研究，尤其是在工具、技术、方法论和人口统计方面的对比，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用定量研究方法，使用Python的Pandas库分析Stack Overflow 2023开发者调查数据，对DevOps专家和普通开发者在技术偏好、人口统计信息和职业经验方面进行对比分析。

Result: DevOps专家和普通开发者在工具和技术偏好上没有显著差异，均使用Docker和Kubernetes等工具；普通开发者的工具使用范围更广以适应不同角色需求；普通开发者更年轻，DevOps专业人员处于职业中期；两类群体都适应远程和混合工作模式。

Conclusion: DevOps专家和普通开发者具有互补角色而非竞争关系，DevOps的重要性日益增长，研究为学术界和行业专业人士理解软件开发角色动态演变提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate the distinct roles of DevOps specialists and general software developers, examining their varying use of tools, technologies, methodologies, and demographics in the current software development environment. In addition, to differentiate these two professional groups regarding their unique contributions and challenges in the field.
  Design/Methodology/Approach: The research uses a quantitative approach to analyze data from the Stack Overflow 2023 Developer Survey. It focuses on a comparative analysis of technological preferences, demographic information, and professional experiences between DevOps specialists and general developers, highlighting key trends and differences. The data analysis was conducted using Python's Pandas library for data analysis.
  Findings: The research indicates no significant difference in the tool and technology preferences between DevOps specialists and general software developers, highlighting their complementary roles. DevOps specialists and general software developers use tools like Docker and Kubernetes, emphasizing efficiency and automation. While general developers employ diverse tools for various role demands, demographic trends show younger general developers and mid-career DevOps professionals. This age range reflects growing experience in DevOps, and both groups are adapting to remote and hybrid work models in the evolving tech industry.
  Practical Implications: This research offers perspectives on the dynamic roles within software development, emphasizing the growing importance of DevOps. It is a valuable resource for academic and industry professionals to understand the evolving dynamics in software development roles.
  Originality/Value: This research fills a significant gap in the existing literature regarding the evolving dynamics of software development roles.

</details>


### [242] [JustDiag!: A Diagnostic Justification Engine for Accountable Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19407)
*Tingzhu Bi,Xinrui Jiang,Xun Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Congjie He,Jinglin Li,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JustDiag是一个面向高可靠性运维场景的根因分析诊断解释引擎，通过维护显式的诊断过程状态（证据、发现、竞争假设、冲突、下一步检查），实现对RCA结果的可问责性。在66个真实案例上的两层次评估表明，相比无诊断解释的基线系统，JustDiag在最终答案质量和过程质量上均表现更优，尽管其因更谨慎的未终结判定而略微降低了任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成流畅的根因分析结果，但仅凭流畅的最终答案不足以支撑高可靠性运维场景下的问责需求。实际事故响应中，工程师需要知道诊断基于哪些证据、考虑了哪些备选方案、存在哪些矛盾，以及系统是否解决了问题或保留了不确定性。

Method: 提出JustDiag诊断解释引擎，维护显式的诊断过程状态，包括证据、发现、竞争假设、冲突和下一步检查。采用两层次评估协议，分别对最终答案质量和过程质量进行评分。

Result: 在66个真实事故案例上，相比无诊断解释的对照组，JustDiag在结果评分和过程评分上均表现更优，尽管其因更校准的未闭环判定而略微降低了任务完成率。

Conclusion: 可问责的根因分析需要显式的诊断解释工件和过程感知的评估方法，而非仅依赖流畅的最终答案。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent root cause analyses, but fluent final answers alone are insufficient evidence for accountability in high-stakes operations. In real incident response, engineers need to know what evidence supported a diagnosis, which alternatives were considered, where contradictions remained, and whether the system resolved the case or preserved uncertainty. We address this gap with JustDiag, a diagnostic justification engine for RCA that maintains an explicit process state over evidence, findings, competing hypotheses, conflicts, and next checks. We evaluated the system on 66 real-world incidents using a two-layer protocol that separately scores final-answer quality and process quality. Relative to a matched control without diagnostic justification, JustDiag achieved stronger outcome and process scores, while accepting slightly lower terminal completion due to more calibrated non-closure. These results suggest that accountable RCA requires explicit diagnostic justification artifacts and process-aware evaluation, not only fluent final answers.

</details>


### [243] [FAPO: Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization of Multi-Step LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19605)
*Paul Kassianik,Baturay Saglam,Huaibo Zhao,Blaine Nelson,Supriti Vijay,Aman Priyanshu,Amin Karbasi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FAPO 是一个让 Claude Code 自动优化多步骤 LLM 管道的框架，它先尝试 prompt 优化，在归因发现结构性瓶颈时才改变链结构。在 18 个模型-基准比较中 15 个优于基线 GEPA，平均提升 +14.1 pp，当升级到结构变化时平均提升达 +33.8 pp，在安全任务上也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步骤 LLM 管道的错误来源于检索、推理和格式化步骤之间的交互，仅靠 prompt 优化会遗漏链中的瓶颈，亟需一种能自动诊断并修复管道各环节的框架。

Method: FAPO 在标准化代码库内让 Claude Code 评估管道、检查中间步骤、诊断故障、提出局部修改并反复验证变体以优化得分函数；优先尝试 prompt 编辑，仅当归因识别出结构性瓶颈且 prompt 优化不够时才更改链结构。

Result: 在 6 个基准和 3 个任务模型上，FAPO 在 18 个比较中 15 个击败 GEPA，11 个比较中均值与标准差区间无重叠，平均增益 +14.1 pp；升级到结构变化的 6 个比较中全胜，平均增益 +33.8 pp；安全任务上 CTIBench-RCM 测试准确率提升 +2.0 至 +7.1 pp。

Conclusion: FAPO 是一种面向通用与安全任务的 SOTA 管道优化技术，能够通过分级优化策略（先 prompt 后结构）有效超越现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-step LLM pipelines fail through interactions among retrieval, reasoning, and formatting steps, so prompt-only optimization can miss bottlenecks in the chain. We present FAPO (Fully Autonomous Prompt Optimization), a framework that lets Claude Code optimize an LLM pipeline inside a standardized codebase. FAPO evaluates a pipeline, inspects intermediate steps, diagnoses failures, proposes scoped changes, and validates variants repeatedly to optimize against a score function. It first tries prompt edits and, only when prompt optimization appears insufficient, changes chain structure within the permitted scope when attribution identifies a structural bottleneck. Across six benchmarks and three task models, FAPO beats the baseline GEPA in 15 of 18 model-benchmark comparisons. In 11 model-benchmark comparisons, FAPO wins with non-overlapping mean $\pm$ trial-standard-deviation ranges, and the mean FAPO-GEPA gain is +14.1 pp. In the six HoVer and IFBench comparisons where prompt-first search escalated to structural changes, FAPO wins all six with a mean gain of +33.8 pp. FAPO also improves performance on security tasks: on CTIBench-RCM, a security CVE-to-CWE task, prompt-only FAPO lifts test accuracy by +4.0 pp on GPT-5, +7.1 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct, and +2.0 pp on Foundation-Sec-8B-Reasoning. These results position FAPO as a state-of-the-art pipeline optimization technique for both general-purpose and security-focused tasks.

</details>


### [244] [StaminaBench: Stress-Testing Coding Agents over 100 Interaction Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19613)
*Vlad Sobal,Shuo Yang,Yuting Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StaminaBench是一个衡量编程Agent在多轮交互中耐力的基准测试。实验发现，所有测试模型在5-6轮内失败，传递测试反馈和允许重试可提升12倍表现，好的框架对强模型提升6倍，弱模型无论何种框架均失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注单轮任务解决率，无法反映真实编程场景中Agent需要处理数十到数百次连续变更请求的实际需求。作者希望构建一个衡量编程Agent在多轮交互中耐力的基准测试，更贴近"氛围编码"（vibe-coding）的实际使用模式。

Method: StaminaBench让Agent实现一个REST API服务器，并通过程序化生成的连续变更请求（实验中为100轮）进行测试。测试完全程序化生成，不依赖LLM，保证可复现性和可靠性。变更序列来自硬编码或LLM驱动的采样器，限制在结构化动作空间内确保有效性。Agent和服务器运行在隔离环境中，通过HTTP与基准测试通信，实现黑盒且语言无关的测试。

Result: 所有测试模型在5-6轮内失败；传递测试反馈并允许重试最多可提升12倍的通过轮数；强模型在不同框架间表现差异达6倍，弱模型在任何框架下均失败。

Conclusion: 确认了"氛围编码"风格（不进行彻底测试的编程）会产生大量bug，指出了测试反馈和框架选择对Agent长期任务执行能力的关键影响。

Abstract: We introduce StaminaBench, a benchmark that measures the stamina of coding agents: how many consecutive interaction turns (change requests) they can handle before failing. Unlike the prevailing fraction-of-tasks-solved metric, this matches real vibe-coding where sessions run dozens or hundreds of turns. In StaminaBench, agents implement a REST API server and modify it across a tunable number of procedurally generated follow-up change requests - 100 in our experiments, resulting in codebases of up to 6,000 lines. Tests are generated fully programmatically without LLM involvement, ensuring reproducibility and reliability; change sequences are drawn from either a hardcoded or LLM-driven sampler, both constrained to a structured action space to ensure changes are valid. The agent and the server run in an isolated environment and communicate with the benchmark through HTTP, making testing fully black-box and language-agnostic. We evaluate six agent harnesses paired with seven open-source LLMs across 20 scenarios of 100 turns each and find that: (1) all the tested models fail within 5-6 turns, confirming that vibe-coding-style programming without thorough testing produces bugs; (2) passing test feedback back to the agent and allowing it to retry improves passed turn count by up to 12x; and (3) a good harness is required for strong performance: stronger models exhibit up to a 6x gap between their best and worst harness, while weaker models fail with any harness. We release the benchmark and the generated tasks to enable further research into multi-turn coding agent behavior. Benchmark code and data: github.com/amazon-science/StaminaBench.

</details>


### [245] [Before the Pull Request: Mining Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19616)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出grite——一种基于git的去中心化协调工具，通过记录代理在提交PR前的任务声明、分配和冲突过程，将重复工作从78%降至0%，吞吐量提升3倍以上，并能自动恢复PR历史中不可见的多种协调失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治编码代理生成大量PR，但被接受率低，存在协调与信任缺口，而PR级别的遥测数据无法解释这一现象。作者认为缺失的信号存在于PR之前——即并发代理如何声明、分配和碰撞共享工作的过程。

Method: 开发grite——一个开源的、无需中央服务器、记录存储在git内部的协调基础层。它利用git的仅追加、签名事件日志直接捕获协调过程，所有代理的日志副本收敛到相同状态，不会静默丢失写入。

Result: (i) 共享基础层将重复/冲突工作从78%降至0%，有效吞吐量提升3倍以上；(ii) 所有代理的日志副本一致收敛，无静默写入丢失，而基于文件的追踪器会丢失并发写入；(iii) 日志是可挖掘的工件，能自动恢复冲突编辑、锁饥饿、冗余发现、竞速关闭等失败模式，其中多种在PR历史中不可见。

Conclusion: PR之前的协调过程是理解代理协作失败的关键信号，grite提供了一种去中心化、可验证的解决方案，并开源了数据集、测试框架和挖掘工具包。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open millions of pull requests, yet large-scale studies find their PRs are produced faster but accepted less often - a coordination and trust gap that pull-request-level telemetry cannot explain. We argue the missing signal lives before the PR, in how concurrent agents claim, divide, and collide over shared work. We study this process through grite, our open-source coordination substrate that needs no central server and stores its records inside git itself, so its append-only, signed event log captures the coordination process directly. We show that (i) this shared substrate reduces duplicate and conflicting work at bounded overhead - the share of work that merely re-does a teammate's task falls from 78% to 0% while useful throughput more than triples; (ii) every agent's copy of the log converges to the same state with no write silently dropped, where a file-based tracker loses concurrent writes; and (iii) the log is a mineable artefact from which concrete failure modes - conflicting edits, lock starvation, redundant rediscovery, race-to-close - are automatically recoverable with provenance, several invisible in pull-request history. We release the dataset, harness, and mining toolkit.

</details>


### [246] [Prompt Quality and Pull Request Outcomes: A Stage-Based Empirical Study of LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19644)
*Richard Sserunjogi,Daniel Ogenrwot,John Businge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析265个开发者与ChatGPT的真实交互案例，研究了提示词结构（Context、Specificity、Verification三个维度）对AI辅助软件开发中代码生成、采纳和集成深度的影响，发现不同提示维度在不同工作流阶段发挥差异化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM工具在协作软件工程中日益普及，但提示词结构如何影响下游拉取请求（PR）结果尚不明确。现有研究主要关注对话有用性、生产力或粗粒度的采纳指标，缺乏对提示结构在协作集成行为中作用的深入理解。

Method: 收集265个经人工验证的开发者-ChatGPT交互样本（来自开源PR中的自我报告ChatGPT使用记录）；基于提示工程研究，从Context、Specificity、Verification三个维度对提示结构进行编码；评估LLM辅助标注与人工判断的一致性；分析提示结构对代码生成、采纳和集成深度的影响。

Result: LLM辅助标注在不同维度上与人工判断的一致性存在显著差异：Specificity一致性最稳定，Context被LLM系统性低估，Verification难以一致评估。Specificity和Context与可操作代码生成关联最强；Verification是代码采纳的主要预测因子；Context与集成深度关联最强。

Conclusion: 提示特征在AI辅助软件工程工作流中具有阶段依赖性的差异化效应：上下文基础影响集成深度，任务特异性影响代码生成，可评估性线索影响代码采纳。这为提示工程和AI辅助协作设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly used in collaborative software engineering workflows, yet little is known about how prompt structure influences downstream pull request (PR) outcomes. Prior studies primarily examine conversational helpfulness, productivity, or coarse-grained adoption metrics, leaving the role of prompt structure in collaborative integration behavior insufficiently understood. We analyze 265 manually validated developer-ChatGPT interactions derived from self-admitted ChatGPT usage in open-source pull requests. Building on prior research on developer-facing artifacts and prompt engineering, we operationalize prompt structure using three dimensions: Context, Specificity, and Verification. We first evaluate whether LLM-assisted annotation can reliably reproduce human judgments of prompt structure, finding substantial variation across dimensions and workflow contexts. Specificity shows the most stable agreement with human judgments; Context is systematically under-scored by the LLM; and Verification remains difficult to assess consistently, motivating a hybrid human-LLM annotation strategy. Using this validated framework, we then examine how prompt structure influences actionable code generation, code adoption, and integration depth across AI-assisted PR workflows. Specificity and Context are most strongly associated with actionable code generation; Verification emerges as the primary predictor of code adoption; and integration depth is most strongly associated with Context. Overall, our findings show that prompt characteristics exert distinct, stage-dependent effects across AI-assisted software engineering workflows, influencing downstream adoption and integration through contextual grounding, task specificity, and evaluability cues.

</details>


### [247] [Library-Aware Doubles and Iterative Repair for Large Language Model-Generated Unit Tests in OpenSIL Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19725)
*Ma Toan Bach,Yuchi Zheng,Haingo Razafindranto,Tanvir Alam,Aric Leather,Ranveer Sandhu,Jitesh Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM多智能体管道的自动化单元测试生成工作流，用于AMD的openSIL固件代码库。在76个函数中成功为73个生成可编译的UT，结合行覆盖率引导后平均行覆盖率从73.9%提升至98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低级C固件单元测试在严格构建约束下非常脆弱，缺少头文件、未解析符号和依赖不匹配常导致编译和链接失败，手动编写UT成本高昂。

Method: 提出LLM引导的多智能体管道工作流，包括：自动生成测试脚手架、库感知的stub/mock/fake创建或复用、以及基于构建日志和行覆盖率反馈的迭代编译-分发修复循环。评估了有无行覆盖率引导和RAG增强的配置。

Result: 76个函数中73个生成可编译UT；无行覆盖率引导时平均行覆盖率73.9%；48个函数的子集评估中，行覆盖率引导下达到98.8%，结合向量数据库检索后为94.7%。

Conclusion: 自动生成-修复管道能显著提升受限固件环境的UT创建效率和覆盖率，减少手动调试工作量。

Abstract: Validating changes in low-level C firmware is expensive because unit tests (UTs) are fragile under strict build constraints, where missing headers, unresolved symbols, and dependency mismatches frequently prevent compilation and linking. This study introduces an automated UT authoring workflow for the Open-Source Silicon Initialization Library (openSIL) firmware codebase maintained by Advanced Micro Devices (AMD) that reduces manual effort through a large language model (LLM) guided multi-agent pipeline. The workflow combines automated generation of test scaffolds, library-aware creation or reuse of stubs, mocks, and fakes, and an iterative compile-dispatch repair loop driven by build logs and line-coverage feedback. We evaluate the approach using compilation success, repair iterations, dispatch success, and line coverage, with time, cost, and token usage as secondary measures. Across 76 functions under test, the workflow generated compilable UTs for 73 functions. In a configuration without line coverage guidance or retrieval augmentation, mean line coverage reached 73.9%. On a 48-function subset evaluated under both configurations, mean line coverage reached 98.8% with line-coverage guidance alone and reached 94.7% when combined with vector-database retrieval. Results show that automated generation-and-repair pipelines can substantially improve UT creation efficiency and coverage for constrained firmware environments while reducing manual debugging effort.

</details>


### [248] [Agentic Electronic Design Automation: A Handoff Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19795)
*Jiawei Liu,Peiyi Han,Yuntao Lu,Su Zheng,Fengyu Yan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以“交接有效性”为核心原则，综述了82个EDA智能体系统，将其分为阶段边界、流程边界和组织边界三类，并提出了五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP），旨在为可信智能体EDA建立通用词汇和研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA流程本质上是多阶段、多交接的，设计产物、脚本和工程决策在工具、会话和组织之间传递，每次传递都携带隐式和显式需求，而阶段局部检查可能无法完全捕获这些需求。LLM智能体的引入使得这些交接更加频繁和复杂，因此需要系统性地研究如何确保交接的有效性。

Method: 综述82个系统，将交接有效性作为组织原则，按边界类型（阶段边界、流程边界、组织边界）分类，分析每类的交接合约、交接对象、协调机制和开放问题，并据此提出五层EDA智能体通信协议（EACP）。

Result: 识别出三类边界系统：Stage-Bound（单一阶段内建立有效性）、Flow-Bound（跨工具/调用/会话保持工作流状态一致性）、Organization-Bound（跨知识和权限边界维护来源、溯源、范围和可采信性）。提出EACP五层协议：智能体发现、智能体消息、工具调用、工作流编排、安全与IP协议。

Conclusion: 本文为可信智能体EDA提供了统一术语和研究路线图，指出交接有效性是确保LLM智能体在EDA多阶段流程中可靠工作的关键，EACP协议为该领域未来的标准化和安全性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electronic design automation (EDA) is inherently multi-stage and handoff-heavy. Design artifacts, flow scripts, and engineering decisions cross tool, session, and organizational boundaries before final implementation, signoff, or release. Each transfer carries explicit and implicit requirements that may not be fully captured by stage-local checks. LLM-based agents now invoke EDA tools directly, embed retrieved knowledge in executable scripts, and hand off state across sessions and stages. Once their outputs condition downstream engineering decisions, the transferred object must satisfy a handoff contract and meet the assumptions of its next consumer. This survey introduces handoff validity as its organizing principle. A handoff is valid when the transferred object satisfies the consumer's acceptance conditions and carries sufficient context, evidence, and provenance for downstream use. We review 82 systems and classify them into three boundary classes. Stage-Bound systems establish validity within a single EDA stage or bounded verification task. Flow-Bound systems preserve coherent workflow state across tools, invocations, and sessions. Organization-Bound systems maintain source grounding, provenance, scope, and admissibility across knowledge and authority boundaries. For each class, we analyze handoff contracts, handoff objects, coordination mechanisms, and open questions. These analyses motivate a five-layer EDA agent communication protocol (EACP), covering the agent discovery, agent message, tool invocation, workflow orchestration, and security and IP protocols. We aim to provide a common vocabulary and research agenda for trustworthy agentic EDA.

</details>


### [249] [The Hidden Environmental Cost of Poor Coding Practices in TensorFlow and Keras Applications: A Study on Resource Leaks and Carbon Emissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19799)
*Bashar Abdallah,Gustavo Santos,Rola Al Bataineh,Alain Abran,Mohammad Hamdaqa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文初步实证研究了两种资源泄漏异味（Improper Model Reuse和Unreleased Tensor References）在TensorFlow和Keras工作负载中对能耗和CO₂排放的影响。实验表明，IMR和UTR分别使电力消耗增加约32%和46%，碳排放相应增加，且统计显著，说明资源泄漏异味会降低ML能效和环境可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管资源泄漏可能引入隐藏的能效问题并增加能耗和碳排放，但目前缺乏量化其环境影响的实证证据。本文旨在填补这一空白，初步探究常见资源泄漏异味对ML应用能效和可持续性的实际影响。

Method: 选取两种常见资源泄漏异味（IMR和UTR），在TensorFlow和Keras框架下设计受控实验：对有异味和无异味（基准）的代码执行相同训练任务，测量并比较电力消耗和CO₂排放量，并使用配对统计检验验证差异的显著性。

Result: IMR和UTR分别导致电力消耗增加约32%和46%，CO₂排放成比例增加。配对统计检验表明差异是系统性的且统计显著，证实资源泄漏异味确实会显著降低ML能效并增加环境影响。

Conclusion: 资源泄漏异味对软件质量和可持续性构成可测量的风险，应在ML开发中整合资源生命周期管理和能效优化实践。

Abstract: Efficiency and sustainability are critical considerations in the development and deployment of machine learning (ML) applications. Among the factors influencing sustainability, resource leaks in ML code can introduce hidden inefficiencies that elevate energy consumption and CO2 emissions. Despite this, empirical evidence quantifying their environmental impact remains limited. This emerging results paper presents an initial empirical investigation of two common resource-leak smells, namely Improper Model Reuse (IMR) and Unreleased Tensor References (UTR), and their impact on energy consumption and CO2 emissions in TensorFlow and Keras workloads. Controlled experiments were conducted for each smell by executing identical training tasks while comparing against a smell-free baseline. Our preliminary results show that both smells consistently increase estimated electricity usage and carbon emissions. IMR and UTR increased electricity consumption by approximately 32% and 46%, respectively, with proportional increases in CO2 emissions. Paired statistical tests indicate that these differences are systematic and statistically significant, providing initial empirical evidence that resource-leak smells may degrade ML energy efficiency and environmental sustainability. These findings suggest that resource-leak smells pose measurable risks to both software quality and sustainability, emphasizing the importance of integrating resource-lifecycle management and energy-efficiency considerations into ML development.

</details>


### [250] [CoRaCommit: A VS Code Extension for Commit Message Generation with Exemplar Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19814)
*Chaoran Cai,Bo Xiong,Chong Wang,Lulu He,Peng Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoRaCommit 是一个 VS Code 扩展，通过检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文、并行调用多个大语言模型生成候选提交消息、并基于用户反馈动态推荐最优模型，显著提升了提交消息生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VS Code 提交消息生成扩展直接调用大语言模型基于代码差异生成消息，缺乏相似提交示例作为参考，且不支持用户反馈驱动的模型推荐，导致生成质量不一致。

Method: CoRaCommit 包含三个核心模块：(1) 检索相似提交示例作为提示上下文；(2) 并行调用多个 LLM 生成候选提交消息供比较；(3) 基于用户反馈动态推荐最合适的 LLM。

Result: 在 ApacheCM 数据集 945 个提交上，CoRaCommit 在 BLEU、CIDEr、METEOR 和 ROUGE-L 指标上均优于现有 VS Code 扩展。

Conclusion: 检索增强上下文能够有效提升提交消息生成质量，结合多模型并行生成与用户反馈机制可进一步优化推荐效果。

Abstract: Commit messages are essential textual artifacts that describe the intent behind code changes, and play a critical role in version control, code review, and historical tracking. However, in practice, commit messages are primarily authored manually, which is time-consuming and often results in inconsistent quality and non-uniform expression. Existing VS Code extensions for commit message generation typically directly invoke large language models based on the code diff, without leveraging similar commit exemplars as references, and rarely support user feedback-driven LLM recommendation. To address these limitations, this paper presents CoRaCommit, a VS Code extension that enhances commit message generation by retrieving similar commit exemplars as prompt context, invoking multiple LLMs in parallel for candidate commit message comparison, and dynamically recommending LLMs based on user feedback. Experimental results on 945 commits from the ApacheCM dataset show that CoRaCommit outperforms existing VS Code extensions across BLEU, CIDEr, METEOR, and ROUGE-L metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented context for commit message generation.

</details>


### [251] [JAMER: Project-Level Code Framework Dataset and Benchmark on Professional Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19830)
*Jianwen Sun,Chuanhao Li,Zizhen Li,Yukang Feng,Fanrui Zhang,Yifei Huang,Yu Dai,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于专业游戏引擎（Godot）的项目级游戏代码数据集JamSet和基准测试JamBench，包含8,133个经过验证的游戏项目。通过设计确定性验证流水线，从超过24万个仓库中筛选出高质量项目。基准测试定义了主题驱动生成和代码补全任务，评估了9个前沿模型，发现随着项目规模扩大，模型能力出现显著下降，代码智能体虽能提升编译通过率但无法改善运行时行为质量，表明架构设计而非语法正确性是当前瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动游戏开发在资产生成、游戏设计和网页游戏编码方面取得进展，但专业游戏引擎上的项目级代码工程因缺乏大规模数据集和确定性评估方法而基本未被探索。作者希望通过游戏开发马拉松（Game Jam）社区累积的开源项目来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于Godot引擎的纯文本格式和无头执行模式，设计确定性验证流水线，从文件完整性到运行时行为收集，从超过24万个仓库中提取并验证了8,133个项目。其中300个经人工验证的项目构成JamBench，其余构成JamSet。定义了主题驱动生成和代码补全两类任务，评估流水线结合编译通过率、结构完整性得分(SCS)和行为对齐得分(BAS)。

Result: 评估9个前沿模型发现能力悬崖：运行时通过率从小项目的80.4%骤降至大项目的5.7%(Task2a)。代码智能体虽提升编译率，但未改善运行时行为质量，表明瓶颈在于架构设计而非语法正确性。验证了JamSet作为有效训练数据的价值。

Conclusion: 专业游戏引擎上的项目级代码生成存在显著的能力瓶颈，当前模型在大型项目上的架构设计能力严重不足，JamSet和JamBench为这一方向提供了可靠的数据基础和评估标准。

Abstract: Current AI-driven game development has made substantial progress in asset generation, gameplay design, and web-based game coding, yet project-level code engineering on professional game engines remains largely unexplored due to the absence of large-scale datasets and deterministic evaluation methods. We present JamSet and JamBench, the first project-level game code framework dataset and benchmark built on a professional game engine. Our key insight is that Game Jam competitions, community events where developers build complete games under tight time constraints, yield thousands of open-source projects suitable for this purpose. Building on the Godot engine's text-based format and headless execution mode, we design a deterministic verification pipeline from file integrity to runtime behavior collection, distilling 8,133 verified projects from over 240,000 repositories. Of these, 300 manually verified projects form JamBench; the rest constitute JamSet. JamBench defines theme-driven generation and code completion tasks, evaluated through a pipeline combining compilation pass rates, Structural Completeness Score (SCS), and Behavioral Alignment Score (BAS). Evaluation of 9 frontier models reveals a capability cliff as project scale increases, with runtime pass rates dropping from 80.4% on small projects to 5.7% on large ones (Task2a). Code Agents improve compilation rates yet yield no gains in runtime behavioral quality, indicating that the bottleneck lies in architectural design rather than syntactic correctness. Experiments validate JamSet as effective training data. All data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [252] [Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation with Large Language Models: From Prompting to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19988)
*Shi Chen,Rongcun Wang,Yuan Tian,Xiaoyuan Xie,Wei Song,Rubing Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SolidityBench（5,470个仓库级Solidity智能合约基准）和SolidityScore（面向Solidity领域的语义评估指标），并系统评估了多种代码LLM在仓库级Solidity代码生成任务上的表现。结果表明：通用模型存在结构性缺陷；非参数方法中RAG表现最佳，ICL超过两个示例后因上下文饱和性能下降；监督微调通过将Solidity领域约束内化到模型参数中取得了最大提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在通用代码生成中表现强劲，但高风险的Solidity智能合约领域缺乏针对仓库级代码生成的充分基准与评估指标，现有基准无法满足Solidity在语言层面、安全性和软件工程约束上的严格要求。

Method: 构建包含5,470个仓库级Solidity智能合约及其自然语言描述的基准SolidityBench；提出SolidityScore语义指标，重点评估安全修饰符、合约声明和Solidity特有关键词等关键结构；在零样本、思维链、上下文学习、检索增强生成和监督微调五种范式下评估Qwen2.5-Coder、DeepSeek-Coder和CodeLlama等代表性模型。

Result: 通用模型在仓库级Solidity生成中存在系统性结构缺陷；非参数方法中RAG表现最优，ICL超过两个示例后因上下文饱和性能退化；监督微调在所有方法中提升最大，能将Solidity领域约束内化到模型参数中。

Conclusion: 高质量领域数据结合监督微调是提升LLM生成智能合约可靠性的最有效策略，本研究为仓库级Solidity代码生成提供了全面的基准与评估框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in general-purpose code generation, but their effectiveness in specialized software domains remains underexplored. Solidity smart contracts represent a high-stakes domain where generated code must satisfy strict language-level, security, and software-engineering constraints. Existing benchmarks and metrics remain insufficient for repository-level Solidity generation, where models must synthesize complete contracts from natural language requirements. To address this gap, we introduce SolidityBench, a benchmark of 5,470 repository-level Solidity smart contracts paired with natural language descriptions. We also propose SolidityScore, a Solidity-aware semantic metric that emphasizes domain-critical constructs such as security modifiers, contract declarations, and Solidity-specific keywords. Using this benchmark, we evaluate representative code LLMs, including Qwen2.5-Coder, DeepSeek-Coder, and CodeLlama, across zero-shot prompting, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and supervised fine-tuning. The results show that general-purpose models exhibit systematic structural deficiencies in repository-level Solidity generation. Among non-parametric methods, retrieval-augmented generation performs best, while in-context learning degrades beyond two examples due to context saturation. Supervised fine-tuning achieves the largest improvement by internalizing Solidity-specific constraints into model parameters. Overall, our study provides a comprehensive benchmark for repository-level Solidity code generation and shows that high-quality domain data combined with supervised fine-tuning is the most effective strategy for improving the reliability of LLM-generated smart contracts.

</details>


### [253] [Beyond Static Endpoints: Tool Programs as an Interface for Flexible Agentic Web Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19992)
*Mugeng Liu,Shuoqi Li,Yixuan Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolPro将LLM代理的工具调用意图表示为可执行的工具程序，通过约束引导的程序构建、效果感知的重放和性能策略，在MCP风格服务上实现高达53.4%的延迟降低和96.1%的流量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理调用的Web服务接口多为静态端点，难以表达包含循环、条件分支、连接和重试的长周期工作流，导致多步服务交互效率低下。

Method: ToolPro将工具意图表示为可执行工具程序，结合约束引导的程序构建、效果感知重放（确保状态修改调用精确执行一次）以及基于性能画像的策略（决定程序执行何时优于逐步调用），并在MCP风格服务上使用WebAssembly沙箱实现。

Result: 端到端延迟降低最多53.4%，客户端流量减少最多96.1%，在网络延迟更高和工作流更复杂时收益更大。

Conclusion: ToolPro通过将多步工具调用编译为可执行程序，显著提升了LLM代理在复杂工作流场景下的执行效率，验证了程序化工具意图表示的有效性。

Abstract: In the agentic web era, LLM-based agents increasingly invoke web services as tools, yet most interfaces remain \emph{static endpoints} that poorly express long-horizon workflows with loops, conditionals, joins, and retries. We present ToolPro, which represents an agent's tool intent as an \emph{executable tool program} that compactly encodes multi-step service interactions with explicit effect types. ToolPro combines constraint-guided program construction, effect-aware replay for exactly-once state-modifying calls, and a profile-driven policy that decides when program execution outperforms stepwise calling.
