We instantiate ToolPro over MCP-style services with WebAssembly sandboxing and evaluate it on diverse workflows of real-world applications. ToolPro reduces end-to-end latency by up to 53.4\% and client-side traffic by up to 96.1\%, with larger gains under higher network latency and workflow complexity.

</details>


### [254] [When Lower Privileges Suffice: Investigating Over-Privileged Tool Selection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20023)
*Kaiyue Yang,Yuyan Bu,Jingwei Yi,Yuchi Wang,Biyu Zhou,Juntao Dai,Songlin Hu,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在工具选择中的过度特权问题（over-privileged tool selection），即智能体在已有足够低权限工具可用时仍选择或升级到高权限工具。作者提出ToolPrivBench评估框架，覆盖8个领域和5种风险模式，发现主流LLM智能体普遍存在该问题，且瞬时故障会进一步加剧。通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移到最小特权工具选择，提示级控制缓解效果有限。提出特权感知的后训练防御方法，显著减少不必要的高权限工具使用，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择研究关注安全无关的元数据偏好，忽略了与权限相关的工具选择安全问题。LLM智能体自主选择工具时，不同权限工具的选择直接影响系统安全性，但这一关键问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ToolPrivBench评估框架，包含8个领域和5种重复出现的风险模式；通过实验测量智能体在初始选择和瞬时故障后升级两种场景下的过度特权工具选择行为；提出特权感知的后训练防御方法，训练智能体优先选择足够的低权限工具，仅在必要时升级。

Result: 主流LLM智能体中过度特权工具选择普遍存在，瞬时故障进一步加剧该问题；通用安全对齐无法可靠迁移到最小特权工具选择；提示级控制在瞬时故障场景下缓解效果有限；提出的后训练防御方法能大幅减少不必要的高权限工具使用，同时保持通用能力。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的工具选择中存在显著的过度特权问题，需要专门的权限感知安全机制而非依赖通用安全对齐来确保最小特权原则的落实。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly select tools autonomously, their choices among tools with different privileges become safety-relevant. However, prior tool-selection studies focus on safety-agnostic metadata preferences, leaving privilege-sensitive choices underexplored. To address this gap, we study over-privileged tool selection, in which an agent selects or escalates to a higher-privilege tool despite a sufficient lower-privilege alternative. We introduce ToolPrivBench to evaluate whether agents choose higher-privilege tools despite sufficient lower-privilege alternatives, measuring both initial selection and escalation after transient tool failures. Across eight domains and five recurring risk patterns, we find that over-privileged tool selection is common among mainstream LLM agents and is further amplified by transient failures. We further find that general safety alignment does not reliably transfer to least-privilege tool choice, while prompt-level controls provide only limited mitigation under transient failures. We therefore introduce a privilege-aware post-training defense that teaches agents to prefer sufficient lower-privilege tools and escalate only when necessary. Our mitigation experiments show that this defense substantially reduces unnecessary high-privilege tool use while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [255] [The Correctness Illusion in LLM-Generated GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20128)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试（KernelBench、TritonBench、GEAK）使用的固定形状allclose检查存在漏洞，构建了24个含LLM转录错误的Triton/CPU内核语料库，提出基于op-schema感知的种子模糊测试+高精度fp64参考+逐(op,dtype)绝对容差的改进方案，在5种GPU上验证该方案能100%识别所有错误内核（10/10），同时零误报通过所有正确控制组（16/16）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成GPU内核基准测试（KernelBench、TritonBench、GEAK）仅通过固定形状、小样本的allclose式检查来评分正确性，这种简化验证方法可能无法有效检测LLM生成的转录错误，需要更鲁棒的评估方案。

Method: 构建包含24个Triton/CPU内核（15个正确+9个LLM风格错误）的受控语料库；采用op-schema感知的种子模糊测试，配合高精度fp64 CPU参考和逐(op,dtype)绝对容差进行重新评估；将语料扩展到26个操作（含flash-attention对），在5种GPU（RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL）上重复验证。

Result: 提出方法在初始语料上正确识别9/9错误内核，15/15正确内核零误报；扩展后在5种GPU上一致识别10/10错误内核（illusion），16/16正确内核全部通过，所有失败均可通过存储种子字节级回放。

Conclusion: 基于op-schema感知的种子模糊测试与高精度参考相结合的方法，能可靠检测LLM生成GPU内核中的转录错误，且跨不同GPU架构结果一致，相比现有固定形状allclose检查具有显著优势。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels (KernelBench, TritonBench, GEAK) score correctness through fixed-shape, small-sample allclose-style checks. The number of inputs varies between benchmarks. The shape, dtype, and tolerance are fixed for each kernel. We test that oracle empirically. We construct a controlled corpus of 24 Triton and CPU stand-in kernels (15 correct controls and 9 LLM-style buggy variants seeded with documented transcription errors) and re-evaluate it under op-schema-aware seeded fuzzing with a high-precision (fp64) CPU reference and per-(op, dtype) absolute tolerances. The seeded oracle flags 9 of 9 buggy kernels and passes 15 of 15 correct controls, at zero precision cost on controls. We extend the corpus to 26 ops (adding a flash-attention pair) and re-run the same protocol on five GPU classes (RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, H100 NVL). The verdicts are identical across all five GPUs: 10 of 10 illusions caught and 16 of 16 controls clean. The corpus result is about LLM-style transcription bugs that the allclose-on-one-shape oracle certifies as correct, not about the bug rate of any specific deployed LLM. Every flagged failure replays byte-for-byte from a stored seed.

</details>


### [256] [Learning Critical Testing Literacy Through Puzzles: an Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20129)
*Niels Doorn,Bart Th. Knaack,Tanja E. J. Vos,Beatriz Marín*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了通过谜题学习批判性测试素养（CTL）的研讨会经验，提出了P4TEST教学框架，并发现谜题本身并非干预手段，完整的解题-汇报-反思序列才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试重要但难以教学，需要有效的教学方法来培养学习者的批判性测试素养。

Method: 基于批判性测试者认知模型，构建了P4TEST教学框架和谜题学习活动知识体系；开展13次研讨会（11次半结构化+2次工作簿/出声思考），收集多维度学习体验数据。

Result: 参与者普遍感觉在进行实验性探索；学生趋于收敛答案，专业人员持续探索；情绪在行为中可见但难以通过书面反思捕捉；出声思考揭示即时推理，书面反思引出元认知反思；形成了"意义建构/行动中反思"这一关键主题。

Conclusion: 谜题本身不是核心干预手段，完整的解题、汇报和反思序列才是有效的教学方式；未来需要更刻意地设计这一序列；开发了带有内置分析功能的开源网络应用以定制研讨会。

Abstract: In this paper, we report our experiences and takeaways from workshops using puzzles to learn CTL.
  Background: Software testing is important yet difficult to teach. We introduced a BoK of puzzle-based learning activities to teach CTL, based on a model of critical tester's cognition, leading to the pedagogical framework P4TEST. We conducted thirteen workshops with students, testers, teachers, and primary school pupils to assess puzzle-based teaching of critical testing literacy.
  Experience: Across eleven workshops, we used a semi-structured approach, varying puzzles, materials, and timing. In two additional workshops, we introduced workbooks and think-aloud sessions to gather more data on the learning experience.
  Observations: Participants consistently perceived themselves as experimenting while solving puzzles. Students tended to converge on solutions, while professionals continued exploring. Emotions were visible in behaviour but hard to surface through written reflection alone. Think-aloud sessions revealed immediate reasoning; written reflections elicited more meta-cognitive reflection. The theme Sensemaking / reflection-in-action captured how participants framed problems, navigated dead ends, and shifted strategies.
  Reflections: Puzzles are not the intervention: the entire sequence of solving, debriefing, and reflecting is. Designing that sequence more deliberately is the work ahead. We also developed an open-source web application with built-in analytics to customise workshops.

</details>


### [257] [N-Version Programming with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20158)
*Javier Ron,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在当代AI编程智能体背景下重新审视了N版本编程的经典概念。通过重现Knight-Leveson经典实验，研究了智能体系统、模型和实现语言的多样性是否会产生多样的故障模式。使用Launch Interceptor Program规范，在共享oracle和100万随机测试输入上评估了48个智能体生成的实现。结果显示了显著的共模故障，且许多共现故障可追溯到规范中特别困难或模糊的部分。同时证明编程智能体的多样性带来了实际收益：在三版本多数投票单元中，平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，且有11,844个N版本单元表现出零观测故障。这是迄今为止最有力的证据，证明基于编程智能体的N版本编程是一种有用的工程策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在AI编程智能体时代，N版本编程（通过智能体系统、模型和实现语言的多样性）是否仍能像Knight-Leveson经典实验所研究的那样，产生多样化的故障模式，从而提升软件可靠性。

Method: 使用Knight-Leveson实验的Launch Interceptor Program规范，生成48个由不同编程智能体（涉及不同系统、模型和实现语言）实现的版本，在共享oracle上进行100万随机测试输入的评估，分析共模故障和多样性的实际收益。

Result: 存在显著的共模故障，许多共现故障可追溯到规范中困难或模糊的部分。但多样性带来实际收益：三版本多数投票单元的平均故障数从单版本的387.44降至130.99，11,844个N版本单元实现零观测故障。

Conclusion: 基于编程智能体的N版本编程是一种有用的工程策略，这是迄今为止支持该结论的最强证据。

Abstract: This paper revisits the classical concept on N-version programming in the setting of contemporary AI coding agents. Revisiting the seminal Knight-Leveson experiment, we study whether diversity across agent systems, models, and implementation languages creates diverse failure modes. Using the Knight-Leveson's, Launch Interceptor Program Specification, we evaluate 48 agent-generated implementations on a shared oracle and a campaign of 1,000,000 randomized test inputs. The results show substantial common-mode failure, along the findings of Knight-Leveson. Further analysis that many of those co-occuring failures can be traced to where is specification is particularly hard or ambiguous. We also demonstrate that diversity from coding agents provides practical benefit: across majority voting three-version units, the mean failure count drops from 387.44 for single versions to 130.99 for triples, and 11,844 N-version units exhibit zero observed failures. Our original results is the strongest evidence to date that N-Version Programming with coding agents is a useful engineering strategy.

</details>


### [258] [Qiskit Code Migration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20173)
*Jose Manuel Suarez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Joaquin Bogado,Alenandro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM与检索增强生成（RAG）的混合方法，利用自动生成的迁移场景分类体系作为结构化知识源，实现Qiskit代码跨版本自动化迁移。实验表明，基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）能显著减少幻觉、提升描述质量，其中Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开发套件（QDK）快速演进带来的技术债务损害了代码可维护性和软件复用；量子软件工程（QSE）领域缺乏高质量训练数据且框架波动性高，通用大语言模型易产生不可靠或幻觉结果。

Method: 提出LLM+RAG混合方法：自动生成迁移场景分类体系作为结构化、版本特定的知识源，结合不同检索方案（无约束与限制性）评估两个LLM（Google Gemini Flash-2.5和OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b），通过自动化可扩展工作流实现。

Result: 基于分类体系的RAG架构（尤其是限制性检索方案）显著减少了幻觉，提升了迁移建议的描述质量；Google Gemini Flash-2.5在检测复杂重构场景方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法能够促进技术独立性，提供抵御API过时风险的智能助手，确保量子算法在快速演变的生态系统中的长期可用性，并降低量子软件工程的学习曲线。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Quantum Development Kits (QDKs) introduces a specific form of technical debt that compromises code maintainability and hinders software reuse. In the specialized domain of Quantum Software Engineering (QSE), this challenge is intensified by the scarcity of high-quality training data and the high volatility of emerging frameworks, which often lead general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) to produce unreliable or hallucinated results. This paper proposes a hybrid approach integrating LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to automate the migration of Qiskit code across versions. The proposed methodology enhances the precision and reliability of migration suggestions by leveraging an automatically generated taxonomy of migration scenarios as the structured, version-specific knowledge source to guide the models. The approach is implemented through an automated, extensible workflow evaluating LLMs (Google Gemini Flash-2.5 and OpenAI Gpt-oss-20b) under different retrieval schemes (unconstrained and restrictive). Results demonstrate that the taxonomy-based RAG architecture, particularly under the restrictive scheme, significantly reduces hallucinations and improves descriptive quality, with Google Gemini Flash-2.5 showing superior performance in detecting complex refactoring scenarios. These findings confirm the potential of this data-centric methodology to foster technological independence and provide robust, intelligent assistants that mitigate API obsolescence, ensuring the long-term availability of quantum algorithms within a rapidly shifting ecosystem and flattening the learning curve within Quantum Software Engineering (QSE).

</details>


### [259] [SysML Modeling of Digital Twins for Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20230)
*Mohammad Samadi,Luís Miguel Pinho,Andrey Sadovykh,Gabriela Lucas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对可再生能源社区（REC）数字孪生建模难题，提出基于模型系统工程（MBSE）的工作流。用SysML在Modelio中重构了REC领域模型（设备分类图+社区组织图），揭示了纯SysML的四个语义缺口，并论证引入SAREF4ENERGY本体作为参考包来填补缺口，为智能能源数字孪生的SysML+SAREF语义融合迈出第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REC数字孪生面临设备、合同、运行时数据异构性挑战，现有建模方法缺乏统一语义支撑。

Method: 从工业验证的REC领域模型出发，在Modelio中用SysML重新表达代表性房屋子集，生成两种Block Definition Diagrams（设备分类图与社区组织图）；随后分析纯SysML的语义缺口，并引入SAREF4ENERGY本体作为参考包闭合缺口。

Result: 构建了SysML表达的设备分类图与社区组织视图，识别出四个语义缺口，提出SAREF4ENERGY本体的导入方案。

Conclusion: SysML与SAREF语义结合用于智能能源数字孪生仍鲜有探索，本文是该方向的首步尝试。

Abstract: Renewable Energy Communities (RECs) are emerging as a key organizational model for local and global sharing of renewable generation, storage, and flexible loads. Engineering Digital Twins of RECs is made difficult by the heterogeneity of devices, contracts, and runtime data involved. In this paper, we take a first step toward a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) workflow for REC's Digital Twins. Starting from an industrially-validated REC domain model, we re-express a representative house subset in SysML using the open-source Modelio tool, yielding two Block Definition Diagrams - a device taxonomy and a community organizational view. We then discuss four semantic gaps that plain SysML leaves open and sketch how the SAREF4ENER ontology could be imported as a reference package to close them. Combining SysML with SAREF-based semantics for smart-energy Digital Twins remains largely unexplored, and we position this paper as a first step along that line.

</details>


### [260] [Phoenix: Safe GitHub Issue Resolution via Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20243)
*Kipngeno Koech,Muhammad Adam,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Joao Barros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Phoenix是一个多智能体LLM系统，通过六个专业智能体（规划器、复现器、编码器、测试器、故障分析器、PR智能体）和七层安全控制，在GitHub问题的分类到PR创建全流程中实现了自动化。在24个实例的SWE-bench Lite子集上达到75%的oracle解决率且无退化，在42个真实问题中实现100%正确性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在自动修复代码时缺乏足够的安全保障和系统化的故障处理机制，尤其是在生产环境下的部署面临WAF过滤、token过期、权限边界、不稳定CI等多个实际挑战。

Method: 采用六智能体协作架构，由基于标签的GitHub webhook状态机协调；引入七层安全控制机制；采用基线感知的测试评估策略，在PR创建前进行基线测试对比；在24个实例的SWE-bench Lite子集和42个真实仓库问题上进行评估。

Result: 在24个实例上达到75%的oracle解决率，无pass-to-pass退化；42个真实问题实现100%正确性保持（CP），硬层级平均122秒。约一半的PR修复定位准确，另一半因规划器定位局限导致代码被放置到错误路径。

Conclusion: Phoenix展示了多智能体系统配合安全控制在自动化缺陷修复中的有效性，但规划器的代码定位能力仍存在局限，需要结合检索增强来解决。同时，论文详细分析了触发各项安全机制的生产部署故障模式。

Abstract: We present Phoenix, a multi-agent LLM system that resolves GitHub issues from triage through pull-request creation, combining seven layered safety controls with a baseline-aware test evaluation strategy. Phoenix decomposes the work across six specialized agents. Planner, reproducer, coder, tester, failure analyst and Pull Request (PR) agent, all coordinated by a label-based GitHub webhook state machine. Every change is checked against a baseline test run before a pull request is opened. On a 24-instance slice of SWE-bench Lite. run on the production webhook path, Phoenix oracle-resolves 75% of instances with no pass-to-pass regressions on successful runs; this curated slice is not directly comparable to full-split leaderboard results, and we discuss the limits of the comparison. A complementary pilot on 42 real issues across 14 repositories yields 100% correctness preservation (CP; mean 122s on the hard tier). Manual inspection shows that about half of the resulting pull requests are well-targeted fixes. The other half place code at incorrect paths, a planner localization limitation we are addressing with retrieval. We also report the deployment failure modes (WAF filtering, token expiry, permission boundaries, flaky CI) that motivated each safety mechanism.

</details>


### [261] [Token-Operations-Oriented Inference Optimization Techniques for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20295)
*Shiguo Lian,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Wen Liu,Minjie Hua,Yutong Liu,Jiangze Yan,Xin Wang,Cong Wang,Yilin Zhang,Yi Shen,Jieyun Huang,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Ping Chen,Xinyu Yang,Kaikai Zhao,Yao Zhao,Xinggang Wang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Junping Du,Tao Chen,Xiang Gao,Qinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文围绕以Token为中心的大模型推理优化，首次提出包含多模型融合、模型优化、计算-模型融合、计算-网络-模型融合的四层技术架构，系统综述各层关键技术及产业现状，分析其在降低Token生产成本、提升服务效率、保障供应稳定性方面的应用价值，推动大模型服务从“可用”到“好用”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推理优化是支撑大规模、低成本、高稳定大模型服务的关键基础，当前亟需系统性的技术框架来指导Token生产成本的降低和服务效率的提升。

Method: 首次提出以Token为中心的四层技术架构：多模型融合、模型优化、计算-模型融合、计算-网络-模型融合，并基于此架构对各层关键技术和产业现状进行系统性综述。

Result: 系统梳理了四层架构中的关键技术及其在实际业务场景中的应用价值，为降低Token生产成本、提高服务效率、保障供应稳定性提供了可行的技术路径。

Conclusion: 本文提出的四层技术架构为大模型推理优化提供了系统性框架，有助于推动大模型服务从仅可调用向可运营转变。

Abstract: Large model inference optimization serves as a key foundation for supporting the scalable, low-cost, and highly stable operation of large model services. Centered on token-oriented inference optimization technology, this paper proposes for the first time a four-layer technical architecture consisting of Multi-model Fusion, Model Optimization, Compute-Model Fusion, and Compute-Network-Model Fusion. It systematically reviews the key technologies and current industry status across these four levels and analyzes the application value of related technologies in real-world business scenarios. This paper provides a practical technical path for reducing token production costs, improving token service efficiency, ensuring the stability of token supply, and driving the transition of large model services from being merely callable to being operable.

</details>


### [262] [A Model-Driven Approach for Developing Families of Reinforcement Learning Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20324)
*Xiaoran Liu,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型驱动的方法，通过混合遗传算法（结合全局搜索与启发式局部搜索）自动生成强化学习训练所需的环境变体家族，并以模型转换形式表达变异和约束，通过模型转换引擎实现搜索过程，在野火缓解场景中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练环境家族开发是劳动密集且易出错的手动过程，难以扩展，亟需自动化方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用模型驱动方法，通过混合遗传算法（全局种群搜索+启发式局部搜索）自动生成环境家族；将变异和约束编码为模型转换，由先进模型转换引擎执行搜索过程。

Result: 在野火缓解场景和课程学习（依赖环境家族的特定学习范式）中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的模型驱动方法能够自动化生成RL训练环境家族，减少人工开销，在课程学习等依赖环境变体的场景具有应用价值。

Abstract: Virtual training environments are software-intensive systems in which reinforcement learning (RL) agents learn, adapt, and demonstrate meaningful behavior. Virtual training environments offer a safe and cost-efficient alternative to training agents in real-world settings. However, to converge, most realistic RL problems require training in multiple, mostly similar but slightly different environments - i.e., families of environment variants. The typical development process of environment families is a labor-intensive and error-prone manual endeavor that does not scale well. To alleviate these issues, in this paper, we propose a model-driven approach for developing families of RL training environments. To obtain the family of environments, we develop an approach and prototype tool. In our approach, a hybrid genetic algorithm - a combination of population-based global search and heuristic local search - generates environment families. Mutations and constraints are expressed as model transformations and are operationalized into a search process by a state-of-the-art model transformation engine. We demonstrate the soundness of our approach in a wildfire mitigation scenario and curriculum learning - a particular learning paradigm that relies on environment families.

</details>


### [263] [AutoPass: Evidence-Guided LLM Agents for Compiler Performance Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20373)
*Zepeng Li,Jie Ren,Zhanyong Tang,Jie Zheng,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoPass是一个多智能体框架，通过向LLM开放编译器内部状态（如优化状态和中间表示），结合运行时反馈迭代优化编译配置，在无需训练/微调的情况下，在x86-64和ARM64上分别实现比LLVM -O3几何平均1.043倍和1.117倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码编译任务中展现潜力，但应用于运行时性能调优面临微架构效应复杂和运行时测量噪声大的挑战。现有的自动调优方案将编译器视为黑盒，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出AutoPass多智能体框架：(1)向LLM开放编译器内部信息，允许查询编译器优化状态和分析中间表示(IR)；(2)使用编译器和运行时证据引导LLM生成优化决策；(3)通过迭代搜索过程，利用运行时反馈诊断性能回归并指导编辑；(4)采用纯推理、免训练方案，无需离线训练或任务特定微调。

Result: 在LLVM编译器上实现，在x86-64和ARM64系统上评估。AutoPass优于专家调优的启发式方法和传统自动调优方法，在x86-64上达到比LLVM -O3几何平均1.043倍加速，在ARM64上达到1.117倍加速。

Conclusion: AutoPass通过开放编译器内部状态给LLM，结合运行时反馈迭代优化，有效解决了LLM在运行时性能调优中的难题，且无需额外训练即可直接应用于新基准和平台。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for code compilation tasks, but applying them to runtime performance tuning is difficult due to complex microarchitectural effects and noisy runtime measurements. We present AutoPass, a multi-agent framework for compiler performance tuning that uses compiler and runtime evidence to guide LLM-generated optimization decisions. Rather than treating the compiler as a black box like prior auto-tuning schemes, AutoPass opens up the compiler to the LLM, enabling it to query compiler-internal optimization states and analyze the intermediate representation to orchestrate compiler options. The search process iteratively refines optimization configurations using measured runtime feedback to diagnose regressions and guide latency-improving edits. AutoPass operates in an inference-only, training-free setting and requires no offline training or task-specific fine-tuning, making it readily applicable to new benchmarks and platforms. We implement AutoPass on the LLVM compiler and evaluate it on server-grade x86-64 and embedded ARM64 systems. AutoPass outperforms expert-tuned heuristics and classical autotuning methods, achieving geometric-mean speedups of 1.043x and 1.117x over LLVM -O3 on x86-64 and ARM64, respectively.

</details>


### [264] [Probe-and-Refine Tuning of Repository Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20512)
*Asa Shepard,Jeannie Albrecht*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“探测与精炼调优”（probe-and-refine tuning）的方法，通过合成bug-fix探测来迭代诊断和修补仓库的指导文件，从而提升LLM编码代理的性能。在SWE-bench Verified上的实验表明，该方法将平均解决率从无指导基线的25.5%提升至33.0%，其改进主要源于覆盖率提升而非精度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理需要仓库层面的操作知识（如文件结构、测试运行、历史失败模式），这些知识不存在于代码本身。工程师通常维护AGENTS.md文件来提供上下文，但关于LLM生成的指导是否有助于代理性能的研究存在争议。本文旨在解决这一争议，并证明指导文件的生成方式是决定性能的关键变量。

Method: 提出probe-and-refine tuning流程：使用合成bug-fix探测（probes）迭代诊断和修补仓库的指导文件，每次通过单次LLM调用完成，无需代理循环或工具使用。该方法在SWE-bench Verified上使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B模型进行了四个独立试验，每试验200步。

Result: Probe-and-refine达到33.0%的平均解决率，优于静态知识库的28.3%和无指导基线的25.5%（p<0.001）。改进源于覆盖率的提升（可评估补丁的实例增加14.5个百分点），而每补丁精度保持统计学恒定（约59%，p=0.119）。步骤预算实验表明指导帮助代理有效利用更大的步骤预算。跨模型实验显示，当模型无法生成足够诊断性输出时，调优循环性能下降，但每补丁精度仍保持恒定。

Conclusion: 指导文件的生成方式（而非是否存在）是编码代理性能的关键决定因素。Probe-and-refine调优能有效提升代理的覆盖率（帮助代理到达正确文件），而非提升其修改质量。该方法依赖模型生成诊断性输出的能力，且指导文件能帮助代理更有效地利用计算资源。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents need higher-level operational knowledge about a repository (which files house which subsystems, how to run the test suite, which workflows have historically led to wrong fixes) that does not exist in the code itself. Engineers typically maintain \texttt{AGENTS.md} files to supply this context as instructions for coding agents, but whether they help is contested: recent studies disagree on whether LLM-generated guidance improves or harms agent performance. In this paper we show that how the guidance is produced is the decisive variable, and introduce \emph{probe-and-refine tuning}: a procedure that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively diagnose and patch a repository's guidance file through single-shot LLM calls, with no agent loop or tool use during tuning. On SWE-bench Verified across four independent trials with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B at 200 steps, probe-and-refine achieves 33.0\,\% mean resolve rate vs.\ 28.3\,\% for the static knowledge base used to initialize it and 25.5\,\% for an unguided baseline ($p < 0.001$ for both probe-and-refine contrasts). The improvement comes from coverage rather than precision: refined guidance produces evaluable patches for 14.5 percentage points (pp) more instances while per-patch precision remains statistically constant ($\sim$59\,\%, $p = 0.119$), showing that improved guidance helps agents reach the correct file rather than improving the quality of the changes they make. Further, a step-budget experiment shows that guidance is what lets the agent use a larger step budget productively, and a cross-model experiment with NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B finds that the tuning loop degrades when the model cannot generate sufficiently diagnostic output, though per-patch precision remains constant even then.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP是一个面向远程内存和分解式内存层级结构的算子优化框架，通过引入传输轮次感知的内存策略，在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接实现了最高97%的传输轮次减少和48%的算子运行时降低，端到端TPC-H和TPC-DS查询平均加速22.7%和26.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程和分解式内存层级扩展了分析型数据库引擎的有效内存容量，但改变了内存不足查询处理的成本结构：远程内存传输不仅涉及数据传送时间，还包含每轮传输的固定往返时延。传统算子分析和缓冲区分配启发式方法主要针对磁盘溢出场景，以最小化总I/O量为目标，在远程内存场景下可能因引发过多传输轮次而导致次优性能。

Method: 将传输轮次纳入延迟成本模型，推导算子特定的缓冲区划分策略；在DuckDB中为阻塞嵌套循环连接、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接实例化该方法，实现传输轮次感知的算子内内存策略。

Result: 在双节点计算-内存测试平台上，溢出密集型微基准测试中传输轮次减少高达97%，算子运行时降低高达48%；端到端测试中，溢出型TPC-H查询平均运行时降低22.7%，TPC-DS查询平均降低26.4%。

Conclusion: 通过感知传输轮次的算子内内存管理策略，REMOP能有效优化远程内存场景下的内存不足执行性能，显著优于传统以最小化总I/O量为目标的磁盘溢出优化方法。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [266] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL是一种扩展SQL的决策查询语言，通过CREATE CANDIDATES和DECIDE两个核心构造，允许用户在关系数据上表达决策问题（包括选项空间定义、策略约束和目标优化），并遵循SQL的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQL无法直接表达决策优化问题（如从关系数据中选择最优选项），用户需要在应用层自行实现优化逻辑，导致决策逻辑分散且难以维护。DeQL的目标是将决策优化能力内嵌到SQL中，使声明式查询语言也能处理最优决策问题。

Method: DeQL通过两个核心扩展实现：1) CREATE CANDIDATES——从关系数据源定义候选选项空间；2) DECIDE——声明决策变量、命名约束和优化目标。语言遵循SQL的设计原则（用户声明什么需优化，引擎决定如何求解；查询输入输出均为关系；问题结构对引擎可见），并支持子集选择、分配、指派、调度等多类决策问题，以及不确定优化、内联模型评分和限时/限质量求解等扩展。

Result: 本文给出了DeQL的完整规范，包括设计原则、语法、形式化文法和执行模型，并通过多个示例（子集选择、分配、指派、调度、多级聚合决策）和扩展功能（不确定优化、内联模型评分、时间质量约束求解）展示了其表达能力。

Conclusion: DeQL是SQL在决策优化领域的重要扩展，核心构造已固定，但语言仍在积极开发中，后续版本将在此基础上进一步完善。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [267] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了向量数据库中细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的愿景，形式化了FGAC策略模型及执行问题，比较了不同执行策略，并指出了未来研究的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库在RAG和AI流水线等安全敏感场景中应用日益广泛，但其安全能力有限，缺乏对细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的完全支持，难以确保数据访问符合用户特定策略。

Method: 形式化定义了向量数据库中的FGAC策略模型及策略执行问题，比较了多种执行策略，并进行了初步实验验证。

Result: 初步发现表明，在保证正确执行FGAC策略、实现高ANN召回率和低查询延迟之间存在固有矛盾，不同执行策略各有优劣。

Conclusion: 政策感知的向量搜索是一个重要的开放研究方向，需要在安全性、召回率和延迟之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [268] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种查询感知的路由框架，通过轻量级ML模型预测各候选方法在给定查询上的召回率，并结合离线基准表选择最佳召回-QPS折衷方案，实现过滤式ANN搜索的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤式ANN方法在不同数据集和谓词类型下表现各异，且同一数据集和谓词下不同查询的最佳方法也不相同，因此需要一个查询感知的自动路由机制。

Method: 构建一个查询感知路由框架：轻量级ML模型预测候选方法的召回率，路由器查询离线基准表（映射方法/参数→召回率与QPS），选择最优召回-QPS折衷方案。通过消融研究将22个候选特征缩减至3个最小特征集，采用回归而非分类作为预测目标以提高精度。

Result: 在6个真实数据集上训练，在5个未曾见过的验证数据集上测试，相比现有过滤式ANN基线，实现了SOTA的召回率与QPS平衡，且延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 查询感知路由是一种有效的策略，能够动态选择最适合当前查询的过滤式ANN方法，实现整体性能最优化。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [269] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出面向数据库系统的双通道通信范式，通过用户态UDP（数据通道）与内核TCP（控制通道）的协同设计，在高速云网络环境下消除内核TCP堆栈的CPU开销瓶颈，实现200 Gbit/s的分布式洗牌和每秒数百万消息的复制键值存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高速云网络中，传统内核TCP堆栈成为数据库性能的CPU瓶颈；用户态UDP虽能降低开销，但需重新实现可靠性与排序等关键保证。亟需一种不将网络视为黑箱、而是与数据库操作协同设计的新方案。

Method: 提出双通道通信范式：1) 高性能数据通道——采用用户态UDP处理延迟敏感和带宽敏感的数据操作；2) 可靠控制通道——采用内核TCP处理协调与恢复等控制操作。利用现代NIC能力，保留TCP的可靠性同时获取UDP的高性能。

Result: 分布式洗牌：三个CPU核即可饱和200 Gbit/s带宽；复制键值存储：每秒处理数百万条消息。

Conclusion: 双通道范式通过数据库与网络的协同设计，在不牺牲可靠性的前提下显著降低了CPU开销，证明了该范式的高效性与简洁性，适用于多种数据库系统场景。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [270] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticDB，一种基于智能体的数据库工作负载重配置框架，通过运行时交互实现DBMS和OS级别的协同调优，在MySQL和PostgreSQL上比最强基线平均提升118.1%性能，并减少22.6%的到达最佳性能时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调优器将数据库调优视为对DBMS knob值的迭代搜索，存在执行成本高、过早缩小配置空间、未能充分解决实际需求（如运行时瓶颈诊断、OS级重配置机会探索、变更鲁棒执行、跨任务经验积累）等问题。

Method: AgenticDB设计了一个上下文驱动的框架，通过提出DBMS和OS级别的变更、在安全约束下应用变更、观察工作负载性能和运行时状态、利用执行反馈指导后续决策，形成自改进的重配置循环过程。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上使用YCSB、Sysbench和TPC-H工作负载进行实验，AgenticDB在所有评估工作负载上取得最佳性能，平均比最强基线提升118.1%，并将到达最佳性能的累计时间减少22.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticDB通过OS级动作空间、鲁棒执行生命周期和内存增强规划，将数据库调优转变为利用运行时反馈迭代改进的自改进重配置过程，实现了更有效和实用的数据库重配置。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


### [271] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对云对象存储场景，提出了以美元成本最优（而非传统未命中率最优）的缓存替换策略基准。通过离线最优策略（多项式时间可解）对比发现：LRU的成本遗憾随异构性增大而上升，GreedyDual可将遗憾降至约十分之一；并给出了是否需要考虑成本的临界条件s* = GET费/带宽费。在真实Twitter轨迹上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缓存优化未命中率的目标在云存储场景下是错误的，因为昂贵的对象即使很少被访问也可能产生巨大开销。缺乏一个基于真实云定价、衡量现有启发式策略与美元最优离线策略差距的公开基准。

Method: 针对均匀大小页面的缓存，使用积分区间线性规划求得精确的离线美元最优解（多项式时间）；针对可变大小页面（NP难），扩展了基于流模型的成本感知离线界（cost-FOO），误差约4%。在真实Twitter轨迹和AWS S3/GCS定价下对比LRU和GreedyDual。

Result: (i) 异构性遗憾律：LRU的美元遗憾与未命中成本离散度高度相关（Spearman 0.87），GreedyDual将其降至约十分之一；(ii) 竞争前沿：当预算恰好覆盖昂贵工作集时，GreedyDual的残余遗憾趋近于零；(iii) 闭式交叉点s* = GET收费/带宽速率（S3约4 KB，GCS约330 B），预测何时需要成本感知缓存。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可复现、计费保真的云缓存成本基准，为缓存策略选择提供了理论指导（临界条件s*），揭示了成本感知缓存在云存储场景下的必要性。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>
